# Wire-or-Cut Decisions

Status: Active

Owners: Product integration and the owning surface

This ledger records decisions for code that implements or calls a product
capability but has no production entry point. The default is to cut dead code.
Each exception must identify a real runtime selection mechanism or a bounded
follow-up ticket.

Desktop entries dated before D-2026-09-15-04 describe the retained Tauri
implementation unless they explicitly name Electron. Words such as “live,”
“mounted,” and “shipped” in those historical decisions do not make a retained
Tauri path a public Desktop capability. Electron is the only public Desktop;
it is Managed Cloud only for inference, reports `localMcp: false`, accepts no
provider key, and has no published installer.

## 2026-07-29: Desktop IPC Baseline

| Capability                                            | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ----------------------------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Desktop planning preview                              | Cut      | `apps/desktop/src/api/planning.ts`, both planning preview components, and `planningStore.ts` were unmounted and invoked nonexistent `agent_preview_*` commands. The live research-plan experience remains in the web chat.                                                                                               |
| Desktop LLM council                                   | Cut      | `apps/desktop/src/api/council.ts` and `councilStore.ts` were referenced only by archived UI and invoked nonexistent `llm_council_query` / `llm_council_get_status` commands.                                                                                                                                             |
| Desktop image gallery/style presets                   | Cut      | The gallery, preset picker, and gallery store had no production import path; their generation call targeted a command that is not part of the native IPC contract. Supported image generation remains on reachable product paths.                                                                                        |
| Desktop slash `execute-plan` handler                  | Cut      | The handler was reachable only from the abandoned planning store and had no mounted chat entry point. Shared slash-command reconciliation is tracked separately in Ticket 1D.                                                                                                                                            |
| Shared subscription wrappers                          | Cut      | Five `desktop-command-client` subscription functions had no consumers and no matching registered native commands. Canonical checkout, plan management, and cancellation remain server-authoritative in the web billing boundary.                                                                                         |
| Shared email credential-migration wrappers            | Cut      | The two wrappers had no consumers and no matching registered native commands. The live database/keychain credential path is unchanged.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Shared voice cancellation/interrupt wrappers          | Cut      | The two wrappers had no consumers and no matching registered native commands. Existing registered voice playback and cancellation commands remain available through their real callers.                                                                                                                                  |
| Renderer-exposed Stripe command set                   | Cut      | Nineteen Tauri command functions had no frontend callers, `billing_initialize` had no startup caller, and both feature branches exposed a phantom API. Local renderer Stripe ownership was removed; web/API billing remains canonical, while the internal `BillingState` used by agent entitlement checks is retained.   |
| Native cloud CRUD and transfer commands               | Cut      | Six `cloud_*` commands were registered fail-closed placeholders that could only return `ERR_CLOUD_NOT_IMPLEMENTED`. The two transfer commands called those placeholders and their TS wrappers had no consumers. Managed Cloud persistence remains on the live shared Web API client boundary in `services/cloudChat.ts`. |
| Legacy native AI project/code commands                | Cut      | Eight uncalled `ai_*` commands duplicated the mounted agent, workspace, and guarded tool paths while keeping project context only in memory. Their two otherwise-unused managed states and web-development mocks were removed with the native module.                                                                    |
| Legacy custom updater command layer                   | Cut      | Five uncalled commands and their unused TS contract duplicated the retained Tauri updater experience. That internal path continues through the signed Tauri updater plugin. Public Electron uses the separate signed-DMG release workflow and currently has no in-place updater feed or published installer.             |
| Orphan native debug/background/GitHub/doctor wrappers | Cut      | Twenty-two uncalled commands owned isolated managed state or renderer mocks but no product entry point. Mounted background agents and Git workflows remain; shared diagnostic checks remain available as Rust internals, while startup recovery retains its separate sanitized diagnostic export.                        |
| Runtime-selected local model list commands            | Keep     | `llm_list_llamacpp_models`, `llm_list_lmstudio_models`, and `llm_list_vllm_models` are selected by provider name at runtime and are explicitly documented in `apps/desktop/wiring-allowlist.json`.                                                                                                                       |
| Remaining temporary Desktop IPC exceptions            | Cut      | The final 52 registrations had no production command-name occurrence. Their native functions remain internal where Rust callers still use them, but they are no longer exported through Tauri; obsolete renderer mocks were removed. No temporary Ticket 1C exception remains.                                           |

The enforcement policy and reviewed exception format are enforced by
`pnpm check:module-reachability` against `apps/desktop/wiring-allowlist.json`.

## 2026-07-30: Enterprise and Gateway Database Boundaries

| Capability                                                | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| --------------------------------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Enterprise configuration and support schema               | Wire     | Migration `0076_enterprise_control_plane_tables.sql` owns SSO/directory configuration, organization policy, audit, usage-ledger, and support-case relations with RLS. Existing Web/admin and gateway routes retain their bounded configuration/support responsibilities.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| First-party SSO sign-in and SCIM provisioning runtime     | Defer    | Configuration storage is real, but authentication/provisioning enforcement is a Phase 3 enterprise product decision. The Admin readiness UI states this boundary explicitly and does not claim that configuration alone enables SSO or SCIM. The unconsumed directory-sync webhook that returned HTTP 501 after signature verification was removed, along with its unused secret contract; no externally addressable provisioning endpoint is advertised until the runtime is implemented. |
| Gateway chat, pairing, and mobile approval relations      | Wire     | Migration `0077_gateway_compatibility_tables.sql` owns the five pre-existing gateway relation names. Reachable routes now use `getUserScopedClient`; the former privileged compatibility allowlist was deleted.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| One-off schema application scripts and Docker init mounts | Cut      | `pnpm db:migrate` is the sole schema authority. It records exact checksums in `schema_migrations`; CI proves clean apply, no-op reapply, drift-free verify, route/relation ownership, and a two-tenant RLS probe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

## 2026-07-30: CLI Placeholder Surface

| Capability                         | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------------------------- | -------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Current-turn backgrounding command | Cut      | `/background` and `/bg` were advertised in the shared registry and both CLI frontends but only returned an unavailable message. They were removed from the registry, TUI, classic shared handler, tests, and command documentation. Subagent batches remain foreground-only until a durable detached-run contract exists.                                                                                                                                                         |
| Placeholder task lifecycle         | Cut      | The six deferred task-registry tools only created empty output files and accepted caller-authored status transitions; no process supplied stdout, exit status, polling, or termination. The tools, aliases, dispatch, in-memory records, `/tasks` command, renderer, help entry, and snapshots were removed. The real foreground `task` subagent tool and approval-gated synchronous shell tools remain. No background process lifecycle is advertised until one actually exists. |

## 2026-07-30: Managed API Key Scopes

| Capability          | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| API key scope grant | Wire     | New keys require one or more of `models:read`, `inference:write`, and `usage:read`; the reachable Account settings picker submits and displays those grants. Legacy empty-scope rows retain only the three public-API grants, never general account-route access. |

## 2026-07-30: Sandbox Boundary

| Capability                         | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ---------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Unverified outer-isolation profile | Cut      | No production caller establishes or verifies an enclosing container, while the CLI previously treated the profile as permission to run without its own OS sandbox. The runtime and Rust/TypeScript protocol variants were removed. Old configuration text now falls back to workspace-write, removed serialized shapes are rejected, and an unrepresentable legacy split-permission projection errors instead of inventing enforcement. Real managed Seatbelt and bubblewrap paths remain. |

## 2026-07-31: Desktop Code-Egress Boundary

| Capability                             | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| -------------------------------------- | -------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Model-authored Desktop code networking | Wire     | The direct command, AGI `CodeExecutor`, and chat `code_execute` now converge on `core/agi/SandboxManager`. Model-owned launches force network access off; macOS applies a Seatbelt network deny profile, Linux applies Bubblewrap `--unshare-net`, and missing/unsupported enforcement refuses the launch. The direct user command alone may request network access through its existing high-risk confirmation. |
| Uncalled Desktop sandbox profiles      | Cut      | `sys/security/sandbox.rs` exposed uninvoked Seatbelt/Bubblewrap/AppContainer profile metadata and all-false feature gates. No runtime caller existed, so the module was deleted instead of retaining a second stub-only sandbox authority.                                                                                                                                                                       |

## 2026-07-30: Accessibility Runtime Boundary

| Capability                   | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ---------------------------- | -------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Orphaned Web a11y utility UI | Cut      | The duplicate standalone utility and unmounted settings panel had no live consumer; the panel's setters were literal no-ops. Both files were removed while the separately used accessible controls and announcement hooks remain.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| System high-contrast support | Wire     | Web and Desktop shipped styles honor `prefers-contrast: more` and Windows `forced-colors: active`, with stronger semantic tokens plus OS colors for controls, links, focus, selection, selected states, and gradient-text fallbacks. Mobile reads Android high-text-contrast and iOS darker-system-colors signals, listens for live changes, and applies static high-contrast light/dark palettes across the shared theme while suppressing custom accents that could weaken contrast. Source and behavior tests pin all three surfaces. |

## 2026-07-30: Crash-reporting Runtime Boundary

| Capability                       | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| -------------------------------- | -------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Desktop native crash-dump upload | Cut      | The optional Rust Sentry feature was compiled out of every release, depended on a runtime shell DSN that packaged GUI apps do not reliably inherit, and initialized before renderer-owned consent/trust-boundary state was available. The separate hand-rolled Sentry HTTP sender also bypassed that authority by accepting automatically queued renderer errors. Both native egress paths were removed. Native panics and submitted diagnostics remain in the redacted local log; a future native uploader requires a typed runtime consent bridge, Managed-mode enforcement, packaged configuration, symbol upload, and end-to-end release verification. |
| Product crash/error reporting    | Wire     | Web and Desktop renderer Sentry paths remain real and fail closed: production/configuration checks, explicit consent, PII scrubbing, and Desktop Local/BYOK rejection precede egress. Mobile, Chrome, VS Code, and Desktop native diagnostics are explicitly local-only and do not bundle dormant upload SDKs. This is the supported surface matrix rather than a promise of universal remote collection.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

## 2026-07-30: Release Accessibility Gates

| Capability                    | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ----------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Automated WCAG 2.1 A/AA audit | Wire     | Desktop registers its rendered cloud-sign-in axe audit as a Playwright project and runs it in the path-scoped blocking E2E job. Web builds and starts the production app, audits five public routes in both light and dark schemes, fails closed on navigation failure, an incomplete run, or any axe violation, and uploads the JSON report. CI guardrails pin both workflow calls and the Desktop project mapping so neither audit can silently become orphaned again. |

## 2026-07-30: Web Conversation Branching Boundary

| Capability                                       | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------------ | -------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Persisted selected-message conversation branches | Wire     | The live Web transcript mounts the shared BranchNavigator and creates owner-scoped managed-cloud branches through `/api/chat/conversations/[id]/branches`. Creation is CSRF-protected, rate-limited, idempotent, bounded, and transactional: it validates the owned fork point, copies the persisted transcript through that message, and records source/target message ids under forced RLS. Mobile remains separately tracked because it still performs only a whole-thread copy. |

## 2026-07-30: Daily-Log Memory Reorganization Boundary

| Capability                          | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ----------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Daily-log memory reorganization job | Cut      | The exposed `compact` step only counted logs and always returned zero created memories. A separate command returned an extraction prompt without invoking an LLM, promotion trusted caller-supplied memory objects, and archive was an independent mutation. No scheduler, model-policy gate, transaction, mounted UI, or production caller made those endpoints a job. The false lifecycle and compaction dashboard claims were removed. Daily-log capture/export/cleanup and saved-memory CRUD/search remain, as does the separate live conversation-context compactor in the local turn host. |

## 2026-08-05: Workflow Marketplace Boundary

| Capability                          | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------------- | -------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Desktop workflow marketplace        | Cut      | A 5,200-line publish/browse/social/templates subsystem with no user path: `stores/marketplaceStore.ts` was the sole importer of `api/marketplace.ts` and nothing imported the store, and every one of the 36 exported functions and 5 exported types had zero consumers. The Rust half (`sys/commands/marketplace.rs` plus the entire `features/workflows/` module tree) was reachable only from that dead chain. A third, independently dead copy lived in `packages/client/desktop-command-client/src/marketplace.ts`, also with no consumers. All three were removed along with the 36 registrations, their `ALLOWED_GENERIC_COMMANDS` entries, and their renderer mocks. The wired `skillMarketplaceStore` skills/plugins catalog is a separate system and is unaffected. |
| Bundled marketplace success stories | Cut      | The template catalog shipped 60 `success_stories` arrays containing 63 fabricated performance statistics presented as customer outcomes ("reduced bugs in production by 60%", "Closed $2M in pipeline", "Saved $100K"), plus `sample_results` claims of the same kind. None were sourced from telemetry. They were invented product copy and were removed with the module rather than scrubbed in place.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

Note on enforcement: these 36 commands passed `check-wiring.mjs` before this cut
because `lib/tauri-mock.ts` and `utils/ipc.ts`, both reachable from
`main.tsx`, named them, which the reachability walk counted as callers. A mock
or an allowlist naming a command can mask it from the orphan check. Prefer
removing the mock entry in the same change that removes a command.

## 2026-08-06 to Wave 2 Orphan Sweep

| Capability                             | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| -------------------------------------- | -------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Legacy research fork                   | Cut      | `ResearchPanel.tsx` declared a parallel type set, called `invoke('research_start')` directly instead of going through `stores/researchStore`, and shadowed `ResearchReport`'s name. `SourceCard`, `ResearchProgressPanel`, and `ResearchSourceCard` were unfeedable: the backend emits `research:source_added`/`research:finding_added` only when `request.task_id` is set (`sys/commands/research.rs:296-322`) and the standalone Deep Research page never sets it. The mounted page now renders `ResearchReport` and `ResearchHistory`.                                                                                                       |
| AGI checkpoint command layer           | Cut      | Fourteen commands with no reachable caller. Three separate dead TS clients (`hooks/useCheckpoints.ts`, `api/agi_checkpoint.ts`, `api/autonomousCheckpoint.ts`) plus five wrappers in `desktop-command-client/src/agent.ts`. Five of the fourteen appeared "called" only because `lib/tauri-mock.ts` named them. The live checkpoint path is `stores/codingCheckpointStore.ts` → `coding_checkpoint_*`, which is untouched. **Follow-up (not done here):** `core/agi/checkpoint_store.rs` and `checkpoint_manager.rs` now have no consumer beyond a doc comment and a re-export; they were left in place rather than silently widening this cut. |
| Desktop visual editor and live preview | Cut      | `features/editing/{VisualEditor,LivePreview}.tsx` had zero importers including their barrel. The mounted Canvas feature owns diff UI and never reused them. Neither contained an `invoke()`, so no Rust went dead. `EnhancedDiffViewer`, `FileTreeWithChanges`, `ChangeSummary`, and `ConflictResolver` remain exported.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Desktop experimental surface           | Cut      | The whole `features/experimental/` directory (6 components) was unreferenced. `KnowledgeBaseViewer` was its only `invoke()` caller.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Duplicate computer-use views           | Cut      | `features/computer-use/{ScreenPreview,ActionLog}.tsx` were subsumed by `ExecutionSidecarScreenView` (which merges computer-use and browser screenshots with source priority) and the type-agnostic `ExecutionSidecarApprovals`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Web reasoning accordion                | Cut      | `ReasoningAccordion.tsx` was an orphaned duplicate of the live `ThinkingBlock`, carrying its own test suite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Web credit alert modal                 | Cut      | `CreditAlertModal.tsx` was imported only by a test. Three reachable warnings already cover the case (`ChatComposerNew.tsx` trial banner, `Usage.tsx` approaching-limit line, and `UsageWarningBanner` above the composer), and the modal required `remainingCents`/`allocatedCents` that no API returns. The no-top-ups/contact-sales product rule it asserted stays covered by `UpgradePlanDialog.test.tsx` and `marketing-copy-regression.test.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Web advanced-mode toggle               | Cut      | `AdvancedModeToggle.tsx` had no importer outside its own test; the `advancedMode`/`advancedModelId` store fields it wrote have no reader. Live model selection is `shared/stores/model-store.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Web connector card                     | Cut      | `features/connectors/components/ConnectorCard.tsx` had zero importers and carried a third copy of `formatRelativeTime` (the live ones are in `ConnectorsPage.tsx` and `shared/utils/format.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

The desktop reachability allowlist ratcheted from 90 entries to 68 across this
sweep. Every removal was forced by the check itself, which fails a stale entry.

## 2026-08-06 to Wave 2 Wire Decisions (Desktop)

| Capability                       | Decision               | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| -------------------------------- | ---------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Deep Research report and history | Wire                   | `DeepResearchPage` showed only a summary and key findings; the report body, its citations, copy, and PDF export existed in `ResearchReport.tsx` taking exactly the store's `ResearchResponse` and had no importer. Both it and `ResearchHistory` (search, delete-one, clear-all, detail dialog) are now mounted.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Execution sidecar                | Wire                   | `ExecutionSidecar` takes no props and self-gates on `isOpen`, but `open()` had NO caller anywhere, so a running agent produced no visual feedback at all. Mounted in `DesktopShellV3` and given an auto-open in `useExecutionSidecarContext` driven by pending approvals, active computer use, browser streaming, or a running tool stream, respecting the store's existing `userClosedThisSession` flag.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Dangerous-tool approvals         | Wire                   | `tool_executor/mod.rs` emitted `approval:request` fire-and-forget and then returned a failed `ToolResult` immediately: the call was already refused before the user saw the prompt, and answering it hit "Approval not pending" because nothing had registered with `ApprovalController`. It now calls `ApprovalController::request_approval` (managed Tauri state, reachable from the app handle it already holds), awaits the decision with a 300s timeout, and proceeds to execute on approval. Fails closed when the controller is unmanaged. Rendering is owned by `ExecutionSidecarApprovals`; `McpToolConfirmationPrompt` keeps `mcp_tool` so one decision never shows two Approve buttons.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Desktop project RAG embeddings   | **Demoted, not wired** | The plan scored this a one-line constructor swap (`RAGEngine::new` -> `with_embeddings`). It is not. (1) `ProjectManager`, the struct the plan named, has zero production consumers; only its own test constructs it. (2) The real call sites are `sys/commands/knowledge.rs:194,231`, but `projectSearchKnowledge`/`projectAddKnowledgeFile` have no consumer either, so the whole chain is unreachable and wiring it would improve nothing a user can reach. (3) Worse, it would introduce a silent bug: the hash fallback is **384-dim** and the configured embedding route is **768-dim**, and the fallback is decided per call, so an Ollama outage between indexing and querying yields dimension-mismatched vectors and `cosine_similarity` returns 0 for every chunk, i.e. search silently returns nothing. Any real implementation must persist the embedding model/dimension per chunk and compare like with like. Recorded here rather than half-done. |

Follow-up surfaced by the above: `core/agi/checkpoint_store.rs` and
`checkpoint_manager.rs` now have no consumer beyond a doc comment and a
re-export, and `features/projects/manager.rs` (`ProjectManager`) is constructed
only by its own test. Both were left in place rather than widening the sweep.

New `HelpSection` links all six; every destination is an existing route.                                                                                                                                                  |

`useChatStream`'s completion path is the prerequisite.

## 2026-08-06: Standing gates

The wire-or-cut default only holds if something mechanical enforces it. Three
gates were added or extended so the UNWIRED and stale-claim classes cannot
silently regrow.

| Gate                                    | State                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| --------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `check-module-reachability`, TypeScript | **New, enforcing.** The check covered Rust only, which is exactly why the UNWIRED class accumulated almost entirely in TypeScript. It now walks the desktop renderer's real import graph from `main.tsx`, reusing the existing `scripts/lib/module-graph.mjs` walker rather than adding a second one. First run found **276 unreachable modules**, seeded as a ratcheting `knownUnreachable` baseline: a NEW orphan fails, and a baseline entry that stops reproducing fails as stale, so wiring or cutting a module forces its line out in the same commit. Both directions verified by deliberately reintroducing each violation. **The 276 are debt to drain, not approvals.** |
| `check-agent-context`, evidence paths   | **New, enforcing.** `workspaceUnits[].evidence` had no existence guard, so the model-registry unit cited a deleted test file for weeks with nothing failing. Matches only concrete paths (slash-bearing, no spaces, no glob characters) because `evidence` legitimately also holds shell commands and globs, a false positive there would push authors to delete real evidence to get green.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `reference-integrity`                   | **Was already failing** before this work (9 undeclared references). Now green, with the ratcheting debt list driven from 228 down to 224.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `knip`                                  | **Configured, deliberately NOT a gate.** First run reports 760 unused files, which is mostly entry-point config tuning (React `lazy()`, Next.js route co-location, adapter-driven panels), not real debt. See `knip.README.md` for the count and the three steps to make it enforcing. A required gate failing with hundreds of untriaged findings gets skipped, and a skipped gate looks like coverage that is not there.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

## 2026-08-06 to Wave 3 (second batch)

| Capability                       | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| -------------------------------- | -------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Desktop temporary chats          | Wire     | `is_temporary` was declared on the wire type, honored by the create route, and purged by the retention cron, but desktop passed a hardcoded `false` at both construction sites, so the capability existed everywhere except a control that could turn it on. Threaded through `ensureCloudConversation`, added a `temporaryChat` preference, and put a toggle in the composer's host controls (Managed Cloud only). The preference is read at CREATION time so toggling it later cannot retroactively change an existing conversation's retention. The settings store is reached by lazy import: a static one drags the whole settings graph into `CloudRuntime` and breaks consumers that mock only its own collaborators. |
| Browser content prompt-injection | Wire     | `PromptInjectionDetector` existed but was applied only to the computer-use screenshot/OCR path; every `browser_get_*` command handed raw page text to the model with no marker and no scan. `browser_get_text`, `browser_get_content`, `browser_get_dom_snapshot`, and `browser_query_all` now wrap results in an explicit untrusted-data fence and prefix a warning on a pattern match, mirroring what the Chrome extension already did in `cdpDriver.ts`. Content is never silently dropped: a false positive must not blind the agent to a legitimate page.                                                                                                                                                              |

## 2026-08-06 to Wave 2/3 final items

| Capability                     | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------ | -------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Edit existing spreadsheet      | Wire     | `ExcelEditor` reads the source workbook through calamine and rewrites it with edits applied, so existing sheets and cells survive. Exposed as `document_edit_excel` across the agent tool catalog, the chat tool dispatcher, and `tool_guard` (RiskLevel::High, always requires approval, validates BOTH the source and destination paths). The LLM tool executor calls the Rust function directly; it is not an IPC command since 2026-10-11.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Edit existing Word document    | **Cut**  | `WordEditor::edit_document` CANNOT parse an existing .docx, docx_rs is write-only, and the function's own `tracing::warn!` says the source "content is not preserved". It builds a new document from the edits alone. Exposing it as "edit your document" would silently destroy the user's file, so it is deliberately NOT registered. The 2.7 plan item assumed both editors were equivalent; they are not. Delete the module or replace docx_rs before revisiting.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Computer-use confirmation wait | Wire     | Superseded 2026-09-05. The pause is no longer owned by the loop's own `is_paused` bool and no longer fails closed. A step the safety layer flags now parks on the channel every other desktop tool confirmation parks on (`ToolConfirmationState::await_confirmation`), rendered on the voice consent dialog through the shared `ToolApprovalRequest` rather than the tool dialog, so one decision keeps one pair of answers. Approval resumes the same step through the same driver, a denial ends the task as `confirmation_denied`, and an unanswered request expires at `CONFIRMATION_TIMEOUT_SECS` (120s) as `confirmation_timed_out`, never as a run. The grant rule is the channel's: `NEVER_REMEMBERABLE` tools always ask, every other one may be remembered for the session only. `ComputerUseTask::require_confirmation`, which nothing ever set, is deleted rather than left as a dead gate. |
| Share-link lifetime            | Wire     | Expiry was hardcoded to 7 days with no caller control. `POST /api/share` now accepts a bounded `expires_in_days` (1, 7, or 30; default 7). Bounded by an enum rather than a free number so a caller cannot mint a link that effectively never expires.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Web artifact version restore   | Wire     | Version browsing was read-only on web while desktop already had rollback. `restoreArtifactVersion` re-upserts the chosen version's content through the shared engine, which APPENDS it as the new latest, versions are content-keyed, so history is preserved rather than rewound. Goes through the shared store directly because its artifact type is wider than web's `ArtifactInput` and narrowing would drop kinds like `component`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

**Remaining deferred, with the blocking reason:** per-message cost and latency
(3.2) needs provider usage plumbed through `useChatStream`'s stream-completion
path into `MessageMetadata` before any widget can render it, no producer sets
`cost`/`tokensUsed` today, so mounting a display first would ship an
always-empty control. That plumbing is a feature, not a wire.

**Remaining for a complete clarify loop:** the card renders read-only today
because `InteractiveCardBlock` passes `ctx: { canRespond: false }` and no server
endpoint accepts an `InteractiveCardResponsePayload`. Answering requires a route
that resolves option ids to labels server-side and satisfies the
exact-set-equality invariant in `claimCloudAgentApprovalCheckpoint`, plus a
producer tool (`ask_clarifying_questions`) that suspends the turn. Until that
lands the card is strictly better than the fallback, the user can read the
questions and options, but cannot be answered in place.
