## 2026-08-06: Conversation printing

| Capability           | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| -------------------- | -------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Print a conversation | Wire     | No print action and no print stylesheet existed. The browser's own Ctrl+P was unusable for two reasons, the second serious: it printed the app chrome, and the transcript is VIRTUALIZED, only the rows currently in the DOM exist, so a long conversation printed as a few pages with the middle silently missing. A partial printout that LOOKS complete is the real failure. `lib/print-conversation.ts` scopes the document via `data-print-scope`, expands the list before printing, and clears the scope on `afterprint` AND on a timer, because `afterprint` does not fire in every browser and a stuck attribute would hide the sidebar on screen. Print styles force ink-on-paper (a printed dark theme is unreadable and wastes toner), avoid breaking messages across pages, and expand link hrefs. |

## 2026-08-06: Project knowledge duplicate detection

| Capability          | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Duplicate detection | Wire     | `checksum_sha256` was computed by the client, stored on the row, and passed to the extractor, but never COMPARED, so re-uploading the same file created a second unrelated row: it consumed one of the project's 20 file slots, doubled the text stuffed into the model's context, and made the file list confusing. Now probed before extraction (so a duplicate never pays for that work) and scoped to non-deleted rows (so re-adding a file the user deliberately removed still works). The conflict is thrown OUTSIDE the try so the schema-not-ready branch can never swallow it. Two existing assertions were rewritten from query-COUNT to intent ("nothing was inserted"), because a count assertion breaks every time the route gains a legitimate read. |

## 2026-08-06: Project knowledge storage quota

| Capability              | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ----------------------- | -------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Aggregate storage quota | Wire     | A per-file byte cap and a 20-files-per-PROJECT count cap existed, but nothing bounded TOTAL bytes, so a user held unbounded storage simply by spreading large files across projects. `knowledgeStorageBytes` joins the plan catalog (free 100 MB, basic 1 GB, pro 10 GB, team 25 GB; Max uncapped, Enterprise negotiated, local-only/BYOK uncapped because they store nothing on the platform), enforced in the knowledge-file upload path by summing across ALL of the user's projects, a per-project quota would have the same hole it is fixing. |

## 2026-08-06: Project knowledge file version history

| Capability           | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| -------------------- | -------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| File version history | Wire     | Migration `0098_project_knowledge_versions.sql` adds `version`, `supersedes_id`, `superseded_at`. Re-uploading a CORRECTED file previously created an unrelated row: the old version stayed active, so the model saw both the stale and the corrected text, the 20-file budget was consumed twice, and nothing said which was current. Same name + different checksum is now an edit that supersedes; same name + same checksum is the duplicate case rejected earlier. `superseded_at` is deliberately NOT `deleted_at`, conflating "replaced" with "user deleted it" would make restoring a version impossible. The previous version is retired only AFTER the replacement row exists, so a failed insert can never leave a project with no active copy. Every "currently in effect" query was scoped: the list, the 20-file count, the duplicate probe, the storage-quota sum, and, most importantly, `project-context-service`, which is what actually feeds the model. |

Note on the migration-ordering guard: `shared-project-knowledge-read-only-migration.test.ts`
asserted that 0090 is the highest-numbered migration touching
`project_knowledge_files` at all, while its own stated rationale is narrower.
"a later migration reinstating a FOR ALL policy would silently undo this". It
was tightened to assert exactly that, so schema evolution is possible without
either renumbering behind an applied migration or deleting a security guard.
Verified in both directions: a probe migration creating a `for all` policy on
that table still fails it.

## 2026-08-06: Jupyter notebook uploads

| Capability       | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ---------------- | -------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `.ipynb` uploads | Wire     | Notebooks could not be uploaded at all. Added `application/x-ipynb+json` to the attachment allowlist with filename inference, and, the part that matters, a dedicated extractor rather than routing them through the plain-text path. A `.ipynb` is JSON, so the text path would have "worked" while feeding the model the notebook's ENTIRE serialized form: base64 PNG outputs, execution counts, kernel metadata, per-cell ids. On a notebook with a few plots that is megabytes of noise that crowds out the analysis and burns the project's context budget. The extractor keeps markdown prose, source, TEXT outputs, and error output (frequently the most informative cell), preserves cell order because a notebook's meaning is sequential, and drops image blobs. |

## 2026-08-06: Project export

| Capability     | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| -------------- | -------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Project export | Wire     | `GET /api/projects/[id]/export` returns a versioned, self-contained JSON snapshot as a download. Only an account-wide GDPR export existed, which is the wrong granularity for moving one project, archiving finished work, or handing a client their material. Deliberately OWNER-ONLY, narrower than `GET` on the project itself: that route also serves org-shared readers, but an export is a bulk extraction of every knowledge file's text, and sharing grants reading in place rather than exfiltration. Includes extracted text (a manifest of filenames the recipient cannot read is not an export), excludes superseded and deleted rows (the export mirrors the project as it stands, not its edit history), and degrades to project-without-files on a pre-migration deployment rather than failing. |

## 2026-08-06: Project duplication

| Capability        | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ----------------- | -------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Duplicate project | Wire     | `POST /api/projects/[id]/duplicate`. Branching a project previously meant recreating its instructions by hand and re-uploading every knowledge file, which is also how the same bytes end up stored twice. Copies settings, instructions, and knowledge files BY REFERENCE to the same storage object (already content-addressed by checksum; re-uploading would double storage for identical content). Does NOT copy conversations: a conversation records something that happened rather than configuration, so copying would fabricate history the user never had, and it is the part of a duplicate nobody can undo by hand. The insert routes through the SAME `assert_user_resource_limit` guard as create, a duplicate that bypassed the project quota would be a trivial way around a paid limit. Owner-only, matching export. Files are copied after the project exists so a quota rejection leaves nothing behind, and a failed file copy still returns the usable project rather than rolling back a successful create. |

## 2026-08-06: Controls that persisted nothing anyone read

Three controls shipped in production that a user could see, and in two cases
operate, while nothing downstream consumed the value. Each is CUT rather than
implemented, because in every case building the missing half would have meant
inventing a policy nobody approved.

| Capability               | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------ | -------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Data retention (days)    | Cut      | `Settings → System` rendered a 1-365 day input that validated and persisted, while `retention_period` had ZERO consumers anywhere in `apps/web/app/api`. Nothing purged anything. A user who set 30 days believed their conversations were being deleted; they were not, which makes this a false privacy claim rather than a missing feature, strictly worse than having no control. Implementing it instead would have meant silently deleting user data on a retention schedule nobody signed off, the same reasoning `0095_published_artifacts.sql:11-20` already records for artifact expiry. The zod schema entry is retained so values stored by the old control still validate; only the input is gone. Re-add when a purge path AND an approved policy both exist. |
| Per-project memory scope | Cut      | `ProjectSettingsDialog` rendered a `<select>` with exactly one option ("Default"), no `onChange`, no state, and no persistence, a dropdown the user could open but never change. It implied per-project memory scoping that exists nowhere: `user_memories` (migration `0010_memory.sql`) has no project column, and `managed-memory-context-service.ts` selects purely by user. The explanatory sentence beneath it is KEPT because it is true and is what a user opening the dialog needs to know: memory is account-wide in both directions.                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `voice` preference       | Cut      | The server-synced `general` preference namespace stored `voice: 'nova'`, an OpenAI TTS voice name, and loaded it into `GeneralSection` state on every mount, where nothing rendered or read it. Web read-aloud runs entirely on the browser's `SpeechSynthesis` engine, which has never heard of that voice set. Replaced by a REAL control (`ReadAloudVoiceRow`) built on `speechSynthesis.getVoices()`, deliberately stored device-locally rather than in the synced namespace: installed voices are a property of the OS and browser, not the account, so a synced `voiceURI` resolves to nothing on the next machine and reads as the setting being ignored. Any `voice` key still stored on an account is ignored.                                                     |

## 2026-08-06: Connector expiry visibility

| Capability                     | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------ | -------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Expired-connector notification | Wire     | `GET /api/connectors` has always returned `needsReauthorization` per row, and `use-connectors.ts:250` has always computed the set, but only `ConnectorsPage` read it. A connector's OAuth grant could expire and the only way to find out was to open that one settings page and scroll to the right row; everywhere else the connector simply stopped working with no explanation. `SettingsModal` gained a surface-owned `navBadges` prop (the shared package renders the badge but never decides what warrants one, so no web policy leaks into the shell Desktop also renders) and `WebSettingsModal` feeds it from the field it was already fetching and discarding. The badge carries a full sentence for assistive tech and the tooltip, a bare "2" says nothing about what needs attention. |

## 2026-08-06: Conversation recovery, camera capture, chat defaults

| Capability                                                           | Decision      | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Restore deleted conversations                                        | Wire          | `DELETE /api/chat/conversations/[id]` only sets `deleted_at`, and there is no conversation equivalent of `cron/purge-deleted-media`, nothing ever purges those rows. Deleted conversations were therefore the worst of both: permanently unreachable (every read filtered `deleted_at is null`) while their messages sat in the table indefinitely. Added a `deleted=only` list filter, `POST /api/chat/conversations/[id]/restore`, and a Settings → Privacy → "Recently deleted" section. Restore deliberately does NOT touch `archived`/`pinned`/`starred` or bump `updated_at`: an archived-then-deleted conversation returns to being archived, and an old conversation returns to its place in history rather than jumping to the top as if it had new activity. The copy makes NO retention promise, because no purge job exists to enforce one, that would repeat the unenforced-retention claim removed from Settings → System the same day. |
| Duplicate conversation                                               | Already ships | The conversation-level "Duplicate as branch" item in `ConversationTitleMenu` forks from the LAST message, and `forkConversation` copies every message up to and including the fork point into a new conversation, which is a complete duplicate. A separate "duplicate" path would be a second implementation of the same behaviour.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Camera capture                                                       | Wire          | `CameraCaptureDialog` in the composer's attachment menu. Deliberately NOT gated on the desktop-only `canTakeScreenshot` capability: `getUserMedia` works in every supported browser, and the camera is the one attachment source a laptop and a phone both have. The preview is required rather than decorative, unlike the screenshot path, where the browser's own picker shows what will be captured, `getUserMedia` returns a live stream with no UI, so grabbing a frame directly would turn the camera light on and attach a photo the user never saw. The stream is stopped on unmount, on cancel, and when it resolves after the dialog has already closed. The preview is mirrored; the SAVED frame is not, because flipping it would reverse any text in shot.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Default model, reasoning effort, text size, code wrapping, shortcuts | Wire          | Settings → General. Default model writes `model-store.ts` and reasoning effort writes `thinking-store.ts`, the stores the composer actually reads, rather than the dead `web-settings-store.defaultModel`/`defaultModelTier` fields a previous attempt targeted. Text size and code wrapping ship WITH their stylesheet hook (`html[data-chat-text-size]`, `html[data-code-block-wrap]` in `globals.css`, stamped by `AppearancePreferences`); the earlier `chatFontSize` field was cut precisely because no CSS rule existed behind it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Keyboard shortcut documentation                                      | Wire          | Three parallel lists existed: the `handleKeyDown` if-chain (what fires), a `shortcuts` array returned from `useKeyboardShortcuts` that no caller read, and a four-entry array in `WebChatPage` that WAS the one the dialog rendered. They had drifted, Escape, Cmd+Shift+C and Cmd+Shift+R all worked and none of them appeared in the shortcuts dialog. Consolidated onto `KEYBOARD_SHORTCUT_DOCS`, now rendered by both the dialog and Settings, with a test pinning docs against bindings in both directions.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Sensitive-data memory exclusions                                     | Wire          | Enforced in `persistManagedAutoMemoryFacts` on the WRITE path, before any candidate reaches `user_memories`, filtering in the UI would leave the fact stored and merely hidden, telling the user it was excluded while it sat in the database and kept being fed to the model. Plain case-insensitive substrings, not regexes: a user-supplied regex is a footgun (`.*` silently excludes everything) and a ReDoS surface on a per-turn server path. Deliberately NOT added to `CLOUD_SAFE_SETTINGS_NAMESPACES`: Desktop's local memory is a different store with a different write path where this filter does not run, so syncing the list would put the terms on a surface that does not honour them. The UI states explicitly that it governs new memories only.                                                                                                                                                                                  |

## 2026-08-06: Project templates

| Capability        | Decision             | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ----------------- | -------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Project templates | Wire (built-in only) | `features/projects/data/project-templates.ts` + a picker in `CreateProjectDialog`. Deliberately NO user-defined template CRUD: the product already has that feature under another name, `POST /api/projects/[id]/duplicate` copies a project's instructions, settings, and knowledge files while leaving conversations behind, which is exactly what "save this project as a template" would do. A second subsystem storing name + instructions would be a parallel implementation with its own table and its own drift. What duplicate CANNOT serve is the empty state, a first project, where the user faces a blank Instructions box and no idea what belongs in it, and that is the gap these fill. Templates only pre-fill fields the create form already sends, so a template can never produce a project the user could not have typed by hand, and the name field is never overwritten once the user has typed into it. |

## 2026-08-06: Developer API: structured outputs and embeddings

| Capability                                      | Decision            | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `response_format: json_object`                  | Wire                | Previously refused, honestly, because the field was validated and read nowhere, a caller could ask for JSON, get 200 OK and prose, and fail in their own parser with nothing pointing at the cause. Now REAL in both halves: `applyJsonObjectMode` appends a directive, and `extractJsonObject` (`lib/json-object-mode.ts`) parses the completion in the non-streaming response path before it is returned, so a 200 means the body genuinely parses as a JSON object. The extractor unwraps code fences and surrounding prose but deliberately NEVER repairs malformed JSON, guessing at a missing brace would hand the caller a document the model did not produce. A completion that cannot be parsed returns 502 with an actionable message rather than prose.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `response_format: json_object` + `stream: true` | Cut (refused)       | A stream hands the caller bytes as they arrive, so by the time the payload could be validated it has already been delivered; buffering the whole response to check it would make `stream: true` a lie. Refused at the schema with a message naming the fix (`stream: false`), which is the only option that does not silently break one promise to keep the other.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `response_format: json_schema`                  | Cut (still refused) | Enforcing a caller-supplied schema needs either native per-provider support, which differs in shape and coverage across the providers this gateway routes to, or a validate-and-retry loop spending the caller's money on retries they did not request. The refusal names both alternatives (`json_object`, or `tools` + `tool_choice`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `POST /api/llm/v1/embeddings`                   | Wire                | The catalog already carried an embedding model with no route to reach it. `/v1/models` correctly did NOT advertise it, so this adds a capability rather than repairing a false claim. OpenAI-compatible shape, and NARROWER than OpenAI where it must be: `dimensions` and `encoding_format: 'base64'` are rejected via `.strict()` rather than accepted and ignored, because returning full-length float vectors to a caller who asked for 256 dimensions or base64 surfaces only later as degraded retrieval or a broken decoder. Goes through the same reserve → call → settle billing path as image generation rather than a lighter one, requires `Idempotency-Key`, and settles on EVERY exit including provider failure and a missing provider key, an unfinalized reservation holds quota the caller can neither use nor see. A ragged or short provider batch is rejected outright: misaligned vectors would attach embeddings to the wrong input text, and nothing downstream can detect that. |

## 2026-08-06: Notifications: the first send paths

`NotificationsSection.tsx:22-31` recorded that every push and email toggle had
been removed because "no email sender, no push dispatcher, and no
agent-task-finished listener exists at all", and instructed: "Re-add a group
once its underlying send path actually exists." Both send paths now exist, so
two groups are re-added, and only two.

| Capability                                  | Decision             | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------------------------- | -------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Mobile push delivery                        | Wire                 | `lib/services/push-notification-service.ts`, the repo's first push SEND path. `POST /api/mobile/push-token` had been storing `mobile_devices.push_token` all along with NOTHING reading the column: tokens collected, never used. Sends via Expo in batches of 100, and clears tokens Expo reports as `DeviceNotRegistered`, left in place, a token from an uninstalled app is retried on every future notification forever. The device ROW is kept, because it still records a device the user owns and deleting it would lose the name and platform shown in settings. Never throws: a scheduled task must not be reported failed because a push provider had a bad minute. Token shape is validated locally, so a corrupted column cannot become an outbound request.                                                                                                          |
| Notification email                          | Wire (one channel)   | `lib/services/notification-email-service.ts` on the proven Resend primitive. The transport was EXTRACTED (`sendTransactionalEmail`) rather than copied, two divergent senders is the same drift that produced two secret-pattern lists and three keyboard-shortcut lists in this repo, and the copy always falls behind on retries or timeouts without anyone noticing until mail stops. The 7 existing support-email tests pass unchanged against the extraction. Uses `AGI_NOTIFICATIONS_FROM_EMAIL`, deliberately NOT the support address: a product notification arriving from the support mailbox trains users to reply where nobody reads, and mixes deliverability reputation between a watched address and an unwatched one.                                                                                                                                              |
| Schedule-completion notifications           | Wire                 | `lib/services/schedule-notification-service.ts`, called from `processClaimedScheduleRun` AFTER `finalizeScheduleRun` commits, a notification inside that transaction would hold the connection across a network call, and a rollback would leave the user notified about work that was never recorded. Chosen as the first consumer because it is the one event that genuinely happens while the user is not looking. OPT-IN per channel, failing CLOSED on a settings outage: notifying someone who never enabled it is the one mistake that cannot be taken back. The two channels are independent, a flaky push provider must not cost the user their email. A `cancelled` run sends nothing, because the user stopped it themselves. No task OUTPUT is included in either channel: notifications render on lock screens and email is neither access-controlled nor revocable. |
| Usage, budget, billing, and security emails | Deferred, scoped out | The backlog row listed six email channels and flagged that "billing and security emails carry compliance stakes, scope per channel". A billing email is a financial notice with retention and disclosure obligations; a security email is the channel an attacker most wants to suppress or spoof. Neither should be added as a by-product of wiring schedule notifications. The transport and the opt-in pattern they will need are now in place.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

## 2026-08-27: Declined P1 UI gaps, re-triaged against code

`audit/registers/ui-gaps.csv` reported zero unresolved P1 gaps while 72 P1 rows sat at
`Not Planned`, each declined with a reason of the shape "X is declined
without/until Y". `unresolvedSeverityTally` treats `Not Planned` as resolved, so
the tracker read clean while the work was open. Ten agents re-read every row
against current code, with an adversarial pass over a cross-batch sample that
overturned three calls (`GAP-097`, `GAP-102`, `GAP-053`).

| Capability                                 | Decision    | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 53 declines reversed to `Open`             | Wire        | Each named blocker is a buildable shared contract, now recorded in the row's `owner` column so the decline is traceable to the thing that unblocks it. 22 of the 53 sit behind six primitives: `account-and-session-service` (5), `browser-runtime-owner` (4), `hook-and-mcp-reach` (4), `plugin-registry-hosting` (3), `recording-timeline-asset` (3), `native-desktop-lifecycle` (3). The rest are one or two rows each. |
| 6 rows already built                       | Re-scoped   | `GAP-053` and the five related rows were built in the retained Tauri renderer. They are not evidence that public Electron ships provider-key or Local controls. Public account API-key management remains owned by the hosted web settings surface.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| 13 declines upheld                         | Decline     | Genuine product decisions, not missing contracts. The canonical case is `GAP-032`: a model-training opt-in is refused because customer content is never used for training (`apps/web/app/privacy/page.tsx:133,447`), so the switch would imply a data path that does not exist. `GAP-019` likewise, Mobile is deliberately not the policy authority; Desktop enforces the approval floor.                                  |
| `GAP-102` plugin disable switch            | Cut or wire | Overturned from "already built" on reachability. `enabledPlugins` appears in exactly five places, all inside the component that draws the switch (`SkillsPluginsSettings.tsx:138,434,437,488,696`); no runtime reads it. A toggle that persists nothing is a UI with no runtime behind it, wire it to `plugin-enablement-enforcement` or remove the control.                                                               |
| P1 ratchet                                 | Tightened   | `audit/baselines/ui-gaps-baseline.json` `maxUnresolved.P1` lowered 124 → 53, so `pnpm check:ui-gaps:monotonic` can only fall from here. The former 124 allowed 71 rows of silent regression.                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `subagents` capability cell                | Re-scoped   | The cited `AgentTaskCreator` → `AgentTaskPanel` → Tauri command path is reachable inside the retained Tauri implementation, not shipped public Electron. It therefore cannot close a public Desktop gap. The server-side E2B executor remains separate; Web/Chrome/Mobile remain an honest gap.                                                                                                                            |
| `task` tool on the VS Code app-server      | Wire        | `extension-vscode/src/integrations/localRuntimeClient.ts:740-743` already spawns the CLI runtime that owns `task`, and the extension has a real local workspace, but `apps/cli/src/app_server.rs` exposes no task/subagent wiring. One hop from working; scheduled as a Tier A wiring gap rather than a build.                                                                                                             |
| Swarm completion telemetry                 | Harden      | `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/core/swarm/agent_spawner.rs:592-604` awaits `core.submit_goal(goal)`, receives a goal id, and immediately reports `{"status":"completed"}`. The `succeeded` and `speedup_ratio` figures surfaced by `AgentTaskMonitor` therefore count submissions, not finished work. Scheduled with Tier A.                                                                                                  |
| Git-worktree isolation for swarm subagents | Dormant     | `create_sandbox(use_git_worktree)` exists (`core/agi/sandbox.rs:689`, worktree setup at `:736-753`) but every caller passes `false` (`executor.rs:816`, `sandbox.rs:718`), and `agent_spawner.rs` never touches `SandboxManager`. No swarm subagent currently receives worktree isolation; do not cite it as an active safeguard.                                                                                          |
| Mobile `computer-use` cell                 | Re-scored   | Not "dead", deliberately disabled. `AddToChatSheet.tsx:726-737` renders the row with a lock icon and `status="Desktop"`, gated by a compile-time `FEATURES.computerUse: false` (`lib/v1FeatureFlags.ts:34`), and no native automation code exists anywhere in `apps/mobile/src`. There is no broken call path, because there is no call path.                                                                              |
| `routes` cell                              | Cut (N/A)   | Scored as a Chrome UI-only stub. No such surface exists under either reading: the extension ships two HTML documents total (`apps/extension/manifest.json:77,80`) and every `publish` symbol in it is internal pub/sub. The cell should be N/A, not present-but-unwired.                                                                                                                                                   |

| --------------------------- | -------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `organization_usage_ledger` | Cut (deferred) | `0076_enterprise_control_plane_tables.sql:149-166` carries schema, RLS and a trigger for a margin ledger (`provider_cost_usd`, `charged_amount_usd`, `gross_margin_usd`, `gross_margin_pct`) that duplicates `provider_cost_events`, the COGS ledger `recordSettledProviderCost` actually writes. `organization-usage-service.ts:1-21` documents in its own header that nothing writes to `organization_usage_ledger`, only account and organization erasure reference it.
