## 2026-09-05: Agent runtime audit, P0 slice J

| Capability                                                | Decision      | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `packages/tools/browser-tool` (D-P5-1)                    | Wire (staged) | Zero call sites: `isRunnableComputerAction` (`src/index.ts:344`) reads like the shared computer-use safety gate but no live path reaches it. The two wired drivers are the Chrome extension `cdpDriver.ts` and the desktop `BrowserExecutor`, each with its own allowlist and consent gate, and approval for a live call is decided by `resolveToolCallGate` in the web tool loop. Decision: the package becomes the shared action contract both drivers import (action schema, approval status, destination policy), no driver code in it; that is P5 scope. Until it lands, the README carries a "Not wired" section so a reader cannot mistake its policy for the enforced one. Not deleted: the contract has a named owner and a scheduled slice. |
| Six tool-call shapes, four approval vocabularies (D-P0-5) | Wire          | The tool primitive now exists once, in `crates/agiworkforce-protocol/src/tool_primitive.rs`, generated into `packages/contracts/types/src/generated/protocol` and surfaced as `@agiworkforce/types` `tool-primitive`. Web declares its action classes and destructiveness through it, desktop maps `RiskLevel`, `ToolSafetyTier` and `NEVER_REMEMBERABLE` onto it, the CLI maps its registry `read_only` flag and `ToolResult`, and `execpolicy::Decision` maps to the verdict in the crate that can see both. No executor changed behaviour in this slice; per-surface adoption of the definition, approval and audit records follows.                                                                                                               |

## 2026-10-10: Developer-session worktrees and checkpoints

| Capability                             | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| -------------------------------------- | -------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Developer-session worktrees            | Wire     | Reverses the 2026-07-30 checkpoint boundary for the Electron Desktop only. The app-server advertises `worktrees: false` (`apps/cli/src/app_server/developer_host.rs:376`) while `apps/cli/src/platform/runtime/worktree.rs` already creates, lists and removes worktrees for the CLI. The host moves that code into one shared crate, exposes create, list, delete and apply-to-local over the app-server, and the Tauri copy in `core/llm/tool_executor/worktree_tools.rs` is deleted. |
| Developer-session per-turn checkpoints | Wire     | Replaces the 2026-07-30 "Cut claim": the claim was cut because no runtime existed, not because the capability was unwanted. The host records a git tree checkpoint per turn, exposes per-turn diff, section-level revert and rollback, and advertises `checkpoints: true` only when that RPC exists. Reverting a section asks first and names the files whose changes are lost. The VS Code command-parity contract keeps forbidding these controls until VS Code wires the same RPC.   |

## 2026-10-11: Tauri agents and scheduling inventory

| Capability                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Decision       | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Governance dashboard commands (14)                                                                                                                                                                                       | Wire           | They took `State<'_, Database>`, a type `lib.rs` never managed, so every call from Settings, Privacy was rejected as "state not managed". They now take the managed `AppDatabase` and reach the same connection `approve_operation` already used.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Process reasoning commands (5) and the outcomes dashboards                                                                                                                                                               | Cut            | Same unmanaged state, and a second fault behind it: `ProcessOntology` and `OutcomeTracker` open the database by path with the machine-derived key, which cannot open a main database keyed from the OS key store. `AGICore::with_process_reasoning` has no caller, so nothing writes `outcome_tracking`, and `GoalOutcomes` and `OutcomesDashboard` were exported but never mounted. The engine types were cut later the same day (dead command sweep below).                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Background task queue (`TaskManager`, `bg_*`, `background_task_*`, `task:*`)                                                                                                                                             | Cut            | `register_executor` had no caller, so a submitted task was re-queued forever while the loop polled every 100 ms. The renderer store, the empty Background Tasks section of the Tasks panel and the `agentStore` task section went with it. Four helpers in `operations.rs` asked for `Arc<TaskManager>`, which was never managed, and would have panicked on first use.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Timeout warnings (switch, dialog, banner, `agi_*timeout*`, `timeout_*`, `TimeoutTracker`)                                                                                                                                | Cut            | `start_timeout_warning_loop` had no caller, so `agi:timeout_warning` was never emitted and the dialog could not open; its actions targeted the dead task queue.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Max Task Timeout (Settings, Agents)                                                                                                                                                                                      | Wire           | The slider was synced into a side channel only the dead queue read, while `AGICore::achieve_goal` stopped every goal at a hardcoded five minutes. Goal submission now passes the saved limit as a `max_duration_secs` constraint the engine reads; a goal without the constraint keeps the five-minute bound. Scheduled AI tasks pass the same limit and cancel the agent when it is exceeded.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Orchestrator IPC (`orchestrator_init`, `_init_default`, `_spawn_agent`, `_spawn_parallel`, `_get_agent_status`, `_list_agents`, `_wait_all`, `cancel_agent`)                                                             | Cut            | Only command-client wrappers called them. `ensure_orchestrator` builds the orchestrator on first use for startup and scheduled tasks; `orchestrator_spawn_agent` stays as a Rust function for the MCP server executor. The cancel, cancel-all, cleanup, pause, resume and refresh commands the agent store calls remain.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| AutonomousAgent IPC (`agent_init`, `agent_submit_task`, `agent_get_task_status`, `agent_list_tasks`, `agent_list_trusted_workflows`, `agent_stop`) and `useExtensionEvents`                                              | Cut            | The agent was only ever created inside `agent_init`, which had no caller, and the one `agent_stop` call sat behind a hook nothing mounted. `AutonomousAgent` itself remains: background agents run on it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Teams (19) and presence (6)                                                                                                                                                                                              | Cut            | No renderer caller beyond wrappers and a `PresenceBridge` nothing imported. `features/teams` is deleted; `PresenceManager` stays because the extension bridge's WebSocket server records connections through it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Intent (9) and prompt enhancement (7)                                                                                                                                                                                    | Cut            | Wrapper-only. `core/intent` and `sys/prompt_enhancement` had no other consumer and are deleted with their managed states.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Composer push to background (`background_agent_push`, `background_agent_should_push`)                                                                                                                                    | Cut            | No caller. The run they would start is not fit for a composer entry: it auto-approves every tool, carries no session trust boundary, ignores the conversation it was pushed from, and uses the automation service captured at launch. Agents are still started by the model's tool and by triggers.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Scheduler `workflow` action                                                                                                                                                                                              | Cut (renderer) | Creation was already refused. The renderer type no longer offers it; the Rust variant stays so a stored legacy row still lists, reports why it cannot run, and can be edited or deleted.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Cron triggers on the scheduler engine                                                                                                                                                                                    | Defer          | `core/agent/triggers.rs` keeps its own cron poll. Moving cron triggers onto `scheduler.rs` needs one job per trigger kept in step across two persisted stores, with a migration for stored triggers; that is not a small change.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Error context commands (`get_error_context`, `get_all_error_contexts`, `retry_failed_step`, `skip_failed_step`, `abort_execution`, `clear_error_contexts`, `get_recovery_suggestion`) and `ErrorContextStore`            | Cut            | Seven `#[tauri::command]` functions that were never registered, over a store that was never managed. `sys/error/commands.rs` is deleted; `ErrorContext` and `RecoveryManager` stay for `sys/error/integration.rs`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `extensionEventsStore`                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Cut            | After `useExtensionEvents` went, the store was written by the runtime event listeners and read by nothing. Its three event payload types moved to `types/extensionEvents.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Autonomous task checkpoints (`TaskStorage`, `AutonomousAgent::resume_from_checkpoint`, `set_task_storage`)                                                                                                               | Cut            | `set_task_storage` had no caller, so the agent never held a store, never saved a checkpoint, and `resume_from_checkpoint` had nothing to resume and no caller either. `core/agent/background_tasks.rs` had no other consumer and is deleted with its three never-created tables.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Embedded MCP server tools (`agi_chat`, `agi_execute_skill`, `agi_research`, `agi_run_task`)                                                                                                                              | Wire           | Settings, Connectors starts the server, but ToolGuard held no policy for these four names, so it refused every call; only `agi_bash`, judged as `terminal_execute`, worked. The three that answer with text and run with tools off are now allowed. `agi_run_task` asks in the app on every call, is on `NEVER_REMEMBERABLE` so no stored, session or auto-approve grant answers for it, is refused in Safe and Plan modes, and its goal carries the unattended tool policy.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Autonomous task plan approval (`ApprovalManager`, `ApprovalRule`, the approval branch of `process_task_queue`, `Task.requires_approval`, `Task.auto_approve`, `AgentConfig.auto_approve`, `TaskStatus::WaitingApproval`) | Cut            | No attended task could exist: `submit_task` had one caller, `run_goal`, which always passed auto-approve, and the only `AutonomousAgent` is the background agent's. The branch would have let one plan approval, or a rule matched against the task description, cover every step the model wrote later. Every step now meets the unattended tool policy on its own, in the first plan and in each revision; a revision that adds a step the run could not start with is not adopted; revisions stop at `MAX_PLAN_REVISIONS`; file steps are held to the granted folders and the secrets denylist before the executor runs. `ApprovalController`, which chat approvals use, is unchanged. |
| Autonomous agent polling loop (`AutonomousAgent::start`, `run_autonomous_loop`, `stop`, the stop signal, `check_resource_limits`, `notify_agent_stop`, `AgentConfig.cpu_limit_percent`, `AgentConfig.memory_limit_mb`)   | Cut            | Nothing called `start`: every `agent.start()` in the tree is `BackgroundAgent::start`, which only marks the record running, and no test or trait object reached the loop. Background agents run through `run_goal`, which enforces the iteration and cost limits itself and returns the reason as the run's error. The resource check and the stop notices were called only from the loop, so they never ran.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Trigger builder edits (prompt, approval, schedule, webhook path, file-watcher folder and pattern)                                                                                                                        | Wire           | The form sent the whole trigger and `update_trigger` read only a flat `name`, `enabled`, `prompt`, `model` and `approvalRequired`, so everything nested under `action` and `config` was dropped while the store showed it as saved. The command now checks and stores `action` and `config`, keeps a webhook's token and the trigger's kind, and returns the stored trigger. Creation was also failing: the engine's cron parser needs a seconds field and counts Sunday as 1, so the builder's five-field presets were refused or a day early, and file-watcher fields were named differently on each side.                                                                              |
| Trigger builder model picker                                                                                                                                                                                             | Cut            | `action.model` was never read when a trigger fired: the run goes to a background agent that takes no model. The picker is removed; the stored field stays so older rows load.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Background run stop, pause and resume                                                                                                                                                                                    | Wire           | A run was a detached task behind a polling waiter: cancel, pause and the time limit dropped only the waiter, so steps and model calls went on, resume ran the goal a second time, and five-second polls were counted as steps. The run is now one future under one `RunControl`. A stop drops the work in flight, and the manager reports cancelled, taken over or interrupted only after the run's task has ended. A pause holds the run at its next step and resume goes on from there (`AgentCommand::Resume` is live). The step limit counts steps that ran, and spend is measured from the run's own start.                                                                          |
| Background run result and progress                                                                                                                                                                                       | Wire           | A finished run returned its own goal as its result and its progress never reached the record, so the panel showed step 0 of 1. The result is now the model's closing summary of what the steps returned, with the bounded step outputs kept in the record, and each step updates the record's progress.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Run summary file on the Desktop (`write_agent_summary`)                                                                                                                                                                  | Cut            | Every finished run wrote `~/Desktop/agi-run-*.md`. A file-watcher trigger on the Desktop or home folder fired on that file and started the next run. A run writes no file of its own; the summary lives in the agent record.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `BackgroundAgentManager::complete_agent`, `fail_agent`, `update_progress`, the autonomous agent's task queue and `AgentConfig.max_concurrent_tasks`                                                                      | Cut            | No caller. The run reports its own ending and progress from the one place it ends, and one agent runs one goal, so there was no queue to manage.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `background_agent_start` arguments `working_directory` and `custom_instructions`                                                                                                                                         | Wire           | Both were validated, saved with the run and read by nothing. The instructions now follow the goal in the text the planner reads. The folder is what the run's relative paths are read against, once it is confirmed to lie inside a granted folder when the run starts; a run whose folder is no longer granted does not start and says so. Each file step is still checked against the granted folders on its own.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `background_agent_cleanup`, `BackgroundAgentManager::cleanup_old_agents`, the store's `cleanupAgents`                                                                                                                    | Cut            | No caller, and it could not have worked: it removed only runs held in memory, and launch loads finished runs for one day, so an older row was never seen and never deleted. Finished runs are now dropped from the table at launch and after each run ends: older than 30 days, or beyond the newest 100. Unfinished runs are bounded by the eight places and one parked run per trigger.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Later firings joining a run parked for approval (`AgentProgress.waiting_firings`, `parked_notice_due`)                                                                                                                   | Cut            | Unreachable: a trigger counts its parked run as live and starts nothing while it waits, so no second firing ever arrived to join. The record now holds `awaiting_approval`, and the notice says the trigger starts nothing else until the run is resumed or cancelled.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Stand-in plan when the planner's model call fails (`TaskPlanner::generate_basic_plan`)                                                                                                                                   | Cut            | A failed model call was replaced by a fixed screenshot-and-search plan, which then failed on a missing screen-reading feature and hid the real cause. The run now fails with the router's own error in plain words: which model was asked, what came back, what to do.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Trust boundary of a background run                                                                                                                                                                                       | Wire           | Every model call of a run went to the router with no boundary. A run now records its boundary when it is created and keeps it however the app is switched later: the mode of the chat that started it, or the app's Local or Cloud mode for a trigger. A Cloud run does not start while the app is in Local mode or signed out, and says why. The panel shows the boundary on each run.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Background runs from a BYOK chat                                                                                                                                                                                         | Defer          | Refused when the run is created, with a sentence saying background runs work in Local or AGI Cloud mode; the run is never moved to a local model or AGI Cloud instead. The tool that starts a run is not told which provider and model the chat's turn is using, and the plain router call a run makes returns no provider to record or show, so the provider label BYOK requires cannot be kept. Wiring it needs the turn's provider and model passed to the tool executor.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Take Over on a background run (`background_agent_take_over`, `BackgroundAgentManager::take_over_agent`, `AgentCommand::TakeOver`, the store's `takeOverAgent` and `lastTakeOver`, `background_agent:taken_over`)         | Cut            | It stopped the run and marked it taken over, which is Cancel under another name: nothing read the context it returned, the conversation snapshot in that context was never filled, and the renderer has no way to open a chat with a starting context. The `taken_over` status stays so stored runs still load, and nothing sets it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Standing trust from an agent approval (the `trust` argument of `agent_resolve_approval`, `TrustedWorkflowStore`, `ApprovalController::is_action_trusted`, `list_trusted_workflows`)                                      | Cut            | Approving in the window with `trust: true` wrote a standing grant to `trusted_workflows.json` that approved the same action in that workflow from then on, with no native question, on a channel a page script can answer. No control sent `trust: true` on this path; the folder dialog's remember option goes through the tool-confirmation channel. The argument, the store and the automatic approval it fed are removed, so an approval covers the one action that asked. The file is no longer read.                                                                                                                                                                                |

## 2026-10-11: Tauri extensibility inventory

| Capability                                                                                                                                                                             | Decision  | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Hook arming at launch                                                                                                                                                                  | Wire      | The registry was built only by `hooks_initialize`, which only Settings, Developer called, so hooks did not run until that page was opened. Launch now builds it, and a tool or prompt that arrives first waits for it (ten seconds at most, then it is refused). The approved text of `hooks.yaml` is kept in the app's encrypted settings rather than beside the file. An unchanged file is armed without a question. A file that is new, changed by one byte, unreadable, emptied or removed arms nothing of its own, raises one notification, and waits in Settings under "Hooks are waiting for your approval"; meanwhile the hooks last approved stay armed, so an edit never switches a guard off. With no approved hooks to fall back on, a file that holds a `PreToolUse` or `UserPromptSubmit` hook, or that cannot be read to find out, holds tools and messages until it is approved; a file of observers only just does not run. Nothing asks from inside a chat send. Settings rewrites the file from the armed hooks, never merged with what is on disk, and changes nothing while a file waits. Disarming is asked about the same way as arming: removing an armed `PreToolUse` or `UserPromptSubmit` hook, switching it off, editing it off both events, importing over it, or reloading or approving a file that no longer lists it is asked about in the same native dialog, with the guard's name and what stops being checked, after Settings has explained it through `useConfirmAction`, and the guard stays armed until the answer there is yes. A change that stops no guard is not asked about. An edit is checked first and swapped in place, so a refused edit leaves the old hook armed. Both decisions are taken in a dialog the operating system draws (`tauri_plugin_dialog`, the call shape of `sys/shutdown.rs` `confirm_then_quit`), not in the window: the window renders model output and previews, so a script injected into it could call any command and answer any dialog the page draws, and a hook outlives the chat that planted it. The dialog shows each hook whole, with every line break, tab and hidden character in any field marked so no value can forge a row: its events, command, working folder, environment (names and values, because they are part of what runs), timeout, what an error exit leads to, and whether it is switched on; a hook that replaces an armed one shows what each changed row was. Nothing is shortened: a change that does not fit the dialog whole (2,000 characters on 40 lines) is refused and has to be made in smaller parts, so no hook is armed and no guard stopped unseen. A hook that is switched off is asked about when it is switched on. All native consent dialogs in the crate (hooks, quitting over live work, computer-use consent) go through `sys/native_confirm.rs`: three buttons, Cancel, the consenting one, Close. With two buttons the plugin reports a dialog that failed, was cancelled or was closed as the second label, which is how a consenting label placed second said yes with nobody pressing it; with three, every such ending lands on Close, Return presses Cancel, and only the exact consenting label counts as yes. The yes is good only for the armed set it was given against (otherwise "the hooks changed while you were deciding"), and the approval is recorded inside the same lock as the write. The window's own confirmation channel is no longer used for hooks at all. A change made in Settings is written to`hooks.yaml` first, to a temporary file renamed over it, and armed only after the write succeeded; when the write fails the command returns the error and what was armed stays armed. The dialog's limit is now rows, not characters: a body is measured as it wraps at 40 characters to a row, the width of the narrowest alert it is shown in, and refused past 40 rows; every blank character that is not the ordinary space is marked like the hidden ones, so padding cannot push a row out of view, and the command is the row straight under the hook's name. On macOS the dialog is always a sheet on the main window and is not asked at all when that window cannot be shown, because the windowless alert puts the consenting button in a slot whose Escape behaviour could not be established by reading. Hook decisions, MCP decisions, capability turn-on, always-allow and delete-all each go through a `ConfirmGate`: one dialog at a time; after a no the same request waits 30 seconds and any other request 10, each further no in a row doubles the wait up to 5 minutes, a yes ends the run, and the refusal says how many seconds are left. A no is never remembered past its wait, so a person who cancelled by mistake can approve the same change without restarting. A file that gains a guard holds the gated actions whenever no approved guard is armed, which includes an approved file with no hooks or with observers only. |
| Hook events `SessionStart`, `SessionEnd`, `UserPromptSubmit`, `Approval*`, `Goal*`                                                                                                     | Wire      | Nine of fifteen listed events were never constructed outside tests. `ui/hooks/lifecycle.rs` now raises them: a chat send loads or creates the conversation, starts its session once per launch and waits for the `SessionStart` hooks, then runs the prompt hooks with that session id before the message is recorded (a new conversation whose first prompt is refused is removed; a Temporary chat keeps no conversation, so its prompt hooks get an id made for that one prompt and no session is started), delete, archive and quit end the session, the three confirmation helpers in `tool_confirmation.rs` raise the approval events, and the agent engine's own `agi:goal:*` events map onto one start and exactly one ending per goal. `PreToolUse` and `UserPromptSubmit` run to completion before the tool or the send, every armed hook in turn, and can refuse it. A hook that ran to an exit code decides by the CLI's rules: exit code 2 or a `{"decision":"block"}` on stdout blocks, with the reason given to the model. Everything else on these two events fails closed, which departs from the CLI on purpose, because a guard that did not decide must not read as a guard that allowed: a hook that did not run to completion (refused by the pattern check, left without a sandbox, not spawned, out of time, killed) blocks whatever it is marked, and a hook the pattern check refuses stays armed rather than being dropped at load; a hook that ran and failed with any other code blocks unless it is marked to keep going, which is all that mark decides there. On every other event no failure blocks, and a failed hook not marked to keep going stops the ones after it, as in the CLI. A hook created in Settings starts unmarked; a saved hook keeps its stored choice, and a hand-written entry with no choice reads as marked. Every other event is fired and not waited for.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PreToolUse` on every path that runs a tool or a shell command                                                                                                                         | Wire      | Only the chat tool loop and the agent engine's tool step were gated. Gated now, each once: chat tools, MCP tools included, in `ToolExecutor::execute_tool_call`; agent-engine steps, sub-agents and swarm agents included, in `AGIExecutor::execute_tool_impl`; background and triggered agent actions in `TaskExecutor::execute_step`, under the chat tool names `terminal_execute`, `file_read`, `file_write`, `browser_navigate` where one exists; computer-use steps a tier takes in `TierDispatch::run` and raw input steps in `execute_action`; `execute_terminal_command`, which is the terminal command, the MCP server's `agi_bash` and the Plugins tab's CLI calls; the scheduler's shell and script jobs in `run_shell_with_timeout`; and `mcp_call_tool`. The MCP server's `agi_chat`, `agi_run_task` and `agi_research` reach these same gates. Not gated, because a person drives them from the window and no model does: the PTY terminal, the file, folder, git, database, cloud, productivity and browser panel commands, capture, speech, power, the MCP OAuth and bundle installers, and the hook executor itself.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `UserPromptSubmit` on every path a person's text reaches a model                                                                                                                       | Wire      | Only a saved chat send was gated. Gated now: every chat send through `prepare_send_message`, which is also where regenerate, edit-and-resend, the retry of an interrupted turn, the floating chat, Quick Query and the MCP server's `agi_chat` arrive, Temporary chats included; a message steered into a running reply, in `chat_add_pending_message`, where a refusal is shown with the hook's reason and the message is dropped rather than queued; goals in the four `agi_submit_goal*` commands and in `orchestrator_spawn_agent` (the MCP server's `agi_run_task`); a scheduled task's prompt when the job runs; a trigger's goal when it fires; `research_start` (and so `research_quick` and `agi_research`); the task given to `computer_use_execute_opa_task`; and the request typed into the terminal assistant. Text with no saved conversation is told to the hooks under a session id made for that one prompt, prefixed with where it came from. Not gated, because the app or a model wrote the text and no person did: sub-agent and swarm goals, a goal a chat tool hands to a background agent (its tool call is gated), dictation clean-up through `llm_send_message`, compaction, titles, memory summaries, the terminal assistant's error and command explanations, an MCP server's sampling request, and resuming a goal or agent that was already submitted.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Starting an MCP server, and letting one out of the sandbox                                                                                                                             | Wire      | Both were asked in the window, through the tool-confirmation channel a page script can answer and whose remembered, session and auto approvals covered every later server; `mcp_connect_server` checked neither that the server was switched on nor that a project server was approved, and `mcp_update_config` asked nothing. Now one decision, `launch_standing` in `core/mcp/config.rs`, is asked at load, at connect, at a restart by the app and at a respawn by the engine: the server is switched on, a project server's entry is still what was loaded, and the launch with the files it runs, read afresh, is the one on record. Starting a server takes an `ApprovedLaunch`, which only that decision or a yes just given can make, so no path can start one without it: Settings connect and reconnect and the Enabled switch ask (`launch_approval::ensure_approved`); an extension asks when it is enabled and starts at app launch only if approved; a connector asks when it first connects; a sandbox change or sign-in refresh restarts only an approved launch; nothing connects at app start, on a project switch or from chat. The question is put through `sys/native_confirm` with the launch shown whole by the hooks dialog's rules (command, each argument, environment with credentials withheld, where it starts, the files checked, sandbox, network, the stored secrets it is given; for a remote server its address, what it is sent, header names and whether the certificate is checked), and one that does not fit is refused. A yes is recorded by fingerprint for the user's own servers as it already was for a project's, so an unchanged launch is not asked about again; saving a configuration records nothing, so a new or changed launch stays unstartable until approved. The filesystem server keeps its approval when the app restarts it with folders the user granted elsewhere, since it was running and only its roots changed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Restart of a stopped project MCP server                                                                                                                                                | Wire      | The shared MCP engine restarts a stopped local server from the launch it saved, on the next tool call, and the app passed no check, so a server from a project's MCP file restarted even after that file changed. `ProjectServerRestart` in `core/mcp/config.rs` now backs the engine's `before_respawn` hook: the file is read again, and the server stays down unless its entry is still the one it was started from, still switched on, and still the launch approved for this project. The tool call gets the reason and it is written to the server's log; the next config reload shows the changed entry as off, and turning it on approves it as it is now. Servers the project file does not list, the user's own and the app's extensions, restart as before: they come from the user's settings, which opening a project does not change. A project approval now also pins the content of the files the launch runs code from that a sandboxed command could have written (the shared `pinned_launch_file_digests` step, with the app's `package.json` reader and the consent code's SHA-256), so an edited script behind an unchanged entry turns the server off at the next load and is refused at a restart. A launch that runs no such file hashes as before, so only approvals of launches with pinned files are asked once more. The Enabled switch, which is the approval, names the files it covers. The limits are the shared step's (64 files, 64 MiB each, read without following a link): a file it cannot pin comes back marked, and one marked file means nothing recorded can match, the server is approved one start at a time, and the switch names the file and the reason. The sandbox opt-out keeps its own fingerprint: it reads relative names from the app's folder and hashes files in listing order, so moving it to the shared step would change every stored opt-out. The server list remembers what it last found for each project server and reads a launch file again only when its size or modified time, or anything the launch names, has changed; that memory is for the list alone, holds one project's servers and at most 64, and is never used by an approval, a start or a restart, which always read the files.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Hooks import, export, reload, run counts                                                                                                                                               | Wire      | Settings, Developer, Hooks. Import asks first when it would replace existing hooks, because the backend overwrites `hooks.yaml`. | `hooks_get_config_path`, `hooks_create_example`                                                                                                                                        | Cut       | Wrapper-only. | User skills directory                                                                                                                                                                  | Wire      | `SkillManagerConfig` now defaults to `~/.agiworkforce/skills`, the directory the CLI and the Developer tab already read. Skills an earlier build kept under the OS config directory are moved there at launch, never over an existing entry.
