| Project skills                                                                                                                                                                         | Wire      | Loaded from `<project>/.agiworkforce/skills`, the CLI's path, whenever the project folder is set or restored. Their bodies stay withheld until the user allows them in Settings, Capabilities; see "Consent to a project's skills in the Tauri app" under the 2026-10-11 dead command sweep for where that consent is kept. | Skill IPC `skill_get`, `skill_check_requirements`, `skill_get_context`, `skill_count`, `skill_get_slash_commands`, `skill_parse_slash_command`, `skill_set_workspace`                  | Cut       | Store actions with no caller. `skill_invoke` stays as a Rust function for the MCP server executor. | Settings, Plugins                                                                                                                                                                      | Re-scoped | The tab reads `~/.claude/plugins` and shells out to the `claude` command; no desktop runtime loads those plugins. tab at it is a separate build. The test call goes through `mcp_call_tool`, so the connector permission and the approval dialog still apply. The registry was a hardcoded list, partly of packages that do not exist, beside the live Bundle Registry tab; the health check duplicated the dashboard refresh; `mcp_store_credential` forwarded to | Enabled MCP extensions at launch                                                                                                                                                       | Wire      | `start_enabled_extensions` had no caller and `enable_extension` returned early on the stored status, so an extension left on was neither started at launch nor startable without turning it off first. Launch now starts the ones stored as running or enabled after `mcp_initialize`, which disconnects every server; one that fails is marked `Error` with its reason and the rest still start. A later reload of the configured servers leaves extension servers connected. | MCP execution history, per-tool statistics and tool schemas (`mcp_get_execution_history`, `mcp_get_tool_execution_stats`, `mcp_get_tool_schemas`)                                      | Cut       | Fetched into `mcpStore` on every tool call and rendered by nothing once the unreachable `MCPConnectionStatus` was deleted; `mcp_get_tool_schemas` had no caller at all. The transcript shows each tool call and its result, and the server card shows the server's own log. `McpToolExecutor` no longer records history or statistics.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Standing grants, code-running configuration and secrets a script in the window could take with no native yes                                                                           | Wire      | Each of these was decided by a command the page can call, with at most a confirmation the page draws and can answer. Now: (1) Autopilot, Build after Safe or Plan, and auto-approve switched on each take a yes in `sys::native_confirm` that says what stops being asked; Autopilot turns auto-approve on in Rust and leaving it turns it off; narrowing never asks (`sys/commands/tool_confirmation.rs`). (2) Always allow for a tool, from Settings or from "remember" on a tool prompt, and "remember these folders" on a folder prompt, take a native yes naming the tool or every folder; a no leaves the request pending. A session-scoped approval stays in the window, as each single approval does. (3) A folder is granted only if it is already granted or was returned by the native picker in this run and still resolves to itself (`sys/picked_folders.rs`, command `pick_folder`); `update_allowed_directories` and `mcp_update_filesystem_directories` refuse any other path, and Settings no longer has a typed-path box. (4) Installing an extension reads the archive once, asks natively with its name, ID, version, path and what it replaces, installs from the bytes it read, stops the installed one and withdraws its launch approval; an extension's launch carries a hash of its package and entry files, so replaced code is a launch nobody approved. (5) The filesystem server restarts with new folders only as the launch it was started with (`McpClient::started_launch`, `ApprovedLaunch::continuing`); otherwise it stays stopped until connected and approved again. (6) The page gets a hook's variables by name with the value only in the form an approval may show, an edit keeps the armed hook's environment, and the export is written by Rust to a file picked in the native save dialog. (7) `npm install -g` for a bundle asks natively with the package and version. (8) Trusting a repository's git settings lists each setting, value and file in a native dialog and trusts only that list (`config_guard::trust_repository_config_as_shown`). (9) `extension_get_config` no longer returns secrets; the page gets their names and a save keeps one it did not send. (10) The launch dialog gives each saved key or sign-in a row of its own and, for a remote server, the host it is sent to. (11) Every git command is held to the granted folders or the open project folder by the test `file_read` uses (`file_ops::granted_directories`); the tool executor keeps its own folder check. Rust for all of this is formatted and read, not compiled or run.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

## 2026-10-11: Tauri device features inventory

| Capability                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Wake word control                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Gate     | `wake_phrase_detection_available()` is a compile-time `false`, so Enable always errored. `voice_get_capabilities` now reports `wakeWordAvailable` and Settings, Voice disables the control with the reason, the same way system dictation is presented.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Spoken replies where the system voice is not implemented                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Wire     | Only the macOS arm of `SystemTts` speaks. Read aloud and spoken replies now choose the engine from the capability probe: the configured voice, else the downloaded Piper voice through `voice_tts_speak_local` (macOS, Linux and Windows x64 bundles exist in `speech-artifacts.json`), else the webview voice. Settings shows the engine that will speak, and disables spoken replies and barge-in with the reason when none can. No path calls a voice the platform cannot run.                                  |
| Git fetch, pull and delete branch                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Wire     | `ChangesPanel` gains Fetch, Pull, the distance from the remote, and Delete a branch behind `useConfirmAction`; failures reach the user as a toast. `git_pull` used to force the checkout on a fast-forward, which discarded uncommitted edits, and committed a merge without clearing the merge state. It is now fast-forward only, moves the work tree with a safe checkout before the branch, and refuses a diverged branch without changing anything. Fetch and pull stop at a five-minute deadline and say so. |
| Git create pull request (`git_create_pr`, `git_generate_pr_description`, `git_get_branch_diff_summary`, `git_check_pr_readiness`, `git_current_branch`, `git_default_branch`)                                                                                                                                                                               | Cut      | The workflow runs `gh pr create` through a bare `std::process::Command`: no host-git config guard, no timeout, no confirmation before publishing, and it fails unless the branch was pushed by other means. `PrCreationWorkflow` had no other caller and is deleted with it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Git merge, stash, remotes, conflict resolution (8), `git_create_branch`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Cut      | No renderer or model-tool caller. Branch creation stays wired through `git_checkout_new_branch`. The conflict parser and resolver in `git_executor.rs` had no other caller and are deleted.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Capture preview, capture history, delete, copy to clipboard, capture from clipboard                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Cut      | Quick capture hands the screenshot to the composer and closes, as the reference apps do; a preview step would exist only to host these controls. Stored captures are still removed by the privacy data wipe. `paste_from_clipboard` is deleted with them.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| OCR IPC (8) and `OCRViewer`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Cut      | Text recognition needs the `ocr` cargo feature and a system Tesseract, neither in the default build, so every command returned "not available". The agent's own OCR tool is unaffected.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Voice store actions and their backend: Deepgram streaming, push-to-talk, barge-in status, configure and monitoring, TTS and wake configure, model and voice selection, text injection, the unmounted voice mode                                                                                                                                             | Cut      | No caller. `features/speech/deepgram.rs` and `ptt.rs` are deleted. `voice_inject_text` was a bare typing call, closed behind the dictation gate, that the dictation plan replaces; the coordinator keeps `ensure_text_injection_allowed`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Native dictation capture (`speech_start_recording`, `speech_stop_and_transcribe`, `features/speech/dictation/capture.rs`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Defer    | Plan stage 3 of `docs/specs/desktop-global-voice/spec.md`, tracked as `DESKTOP-SYSTEM-DICTATION-UNWIRED-01`. Recorded in `apps/desktop/wiring-allowlist.json`; the unused renderer wrappers are removed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Language server IPC (17), `useLSP`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Cut      | `CodeEditor` has no language-server client; wiring completion, hover and diagnostics into Monaco is not a small change, and the servers are not bundled.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Media history, the media generation store and progress component                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Cut      | `media_history.json` was written on every generation and read by nothing. Image and video generation by the model is unaffected.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| File IPC with no renderer caller (`file_copy`, `file_move`, `file_metadata`, `file_get_metadata`, `dir_traverse`, `file_read_range`, `file_read_text`, `file_write_text`, `file_read_binary`, `file_write_binary`, `fs_read_file_content`, `fs_get_workspace_files`, `undo_file_operation`, `file_watch_list`, `file_watch_stop_all`) and `filesystemStore` | Cut      | The model's file tools are separate implementations in `core/llm/tool_executor/file_tools.rs` and call none of these.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `execute_code` IPC, `editingStore` and `features/editing`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Cut      | A second entry to the sandbox whose only caller was an unmounted store. The model's code tool reaches `SandboxManager` directly.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `terminal_execute`, `terminal_list_sessions`, terminal session environment commands (4)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Cut      | Store actions with no caller.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `undo_get_summary`, `undo_get_changes`, `undo_change`, `undo_task`, form undo (9)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Cut      | No caller; `core/agent/form_undo.rs` recorded nothing because nothing submitted to it. Undo last change, the model's undo tools and coding checkpoints remain.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

## 2026-10-11: Tauri models and platform inventory

Retained Tauri code only; nothing here changes the public Electron Desktop.

| Capability                                                                                                                                                           | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Ollama model memory: loaded list, load, unload, keep-alive                                                                                                           | Wire     | Nothing called `/api/ps` or sent `keep_alive`. `sys/commands/ollama.rs` now lists loaded models, loads and unloads through a prompt-less `/api/generate`, and saves a keep-alive in `settings_v2` (`llm.ollama_keep_alive_seconds`) that `core/llm/providers/ollama.rs` adds to every chat request. Settings, Models and Keys shows them through `LocalModelMemory.tsx`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| LM Studio load and unload                                                                                                                                            | Wire     | LM Studio 0.4.0 documents `GET /api/v1/models`, `POST /api/v1/models/load` and `/unload`. `llm_lmstudio_*` in `sys/commands/llm.rs` call them at the registered LM Studio URL without its `/v1` suffix; an older server answers 404 and the panel says which version is needed. llama.cpp and vLLM state that it is not offered.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Ollama pull                                                                                                                                                          | Wire     | `ollama_pull_model` sent `stream: false` with a 24 hour timeout, so there was no progress and no way to stop. It now follows the `/api/pull` stream, emits `ollama:pull-progress`, and `ollama_cancel_pull` drops the stream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ollama_get_model_info`, `get_model_capabilities`, `clear_model_capability_cache`                                                                                    | Cut      | No caller. The renderer resolves capabilities from the registry (`TauriRuntime.ts`). The capability cache is now cleared when a model is pulled or deleted.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `settings_v2_get`, `_set`, `_get_batch`, `_get_category`, `_list_all`, `_load_app_settings`, `_save_app_settings`                                                    | Cut      | Wrapper-only. `settings_v2_delete` (computer-use consent) and `settings_v2_clear_cache` (Privacy) stay. The `AppSettings` model and the service methods that existed only for these commands went with them.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Settings fields `favoriteModels`, `showTimestamps`, `enableCheckpointing`, `checkpointInterval`, `autoResumeOnRestart`                                               | Cut      | No reader in Rust or the renderer. A stored value is ignored on load and not written back. `defaultModels.managed_cloud` stays: `modelStore.ts` reads and writes it through a provider-keyed lookup.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `window_maximize`, `window_unmaximize`, `window_is_maximized`, `window_is_fullscreen`, `window_set_fullscreen`, `window_open_floating`, `window_is_floating_visible` | Cut      | Reached only from `windowStore` actions nothing called. Toggle maximise and the floating toggle and close stay.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Stronghold BYOK vault                                                                                                                                                | Cut      | `src/lib/byok-vault.ts` had no importer; provider keys live in `settings_v2`. Plugin init, `stronghold:*` permissions and the Rust test are removed. The fs deny entries for `keys.stronghold` and `stronghold.salt` stay, since earlier installs have those files. The crate and npm dependencies are removed with the next lockfile update.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `master_password_is_configured`, `_is_unlocked`, `_verify`, `_needs_migration`                                                                                       | Cut      | No caller; `master_password_get_status` carries all four answers.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `master_password_migrate_credentials`                                                                                                                                | Wire     | Deregistered on 2026-07-29 for having no caller, which left "Migrate Secrets" re-encrypting nothing. Registered again. It seals only the rows `sys/security/vault_credentials.rs` names as vault-owned, provider API keys (`api_key_*`), reads each new ciphertext back through the readers' own path before replacing the old one, and does it in one transaction. Rows read while the vault is locked stay under the per-install key. Setting a password now seals every saved key itself, so this is only for a store whose password an earlier build set.                                                                  |
| Vault lock                                                                                                                                                           | Wire     | `master_password_lock` cleared the vault key and left the providers it had unsealed in the router, so BYOK calls kept working while locked. It now forgets every provider registered from a saved key and restores only those a locked launch would. Settings shows the others as locked (`llm_list_locked_api_keys`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Master password change and removal                                                                                                                                   | Wire     | `MasterPasswordManager::change` replaced the salt and verifier without touching what the old key had sealed, so a password change left every sealed credential unreadable, and there was no way to remove the password short of erasing all local data. Change and the new `remove` now run a re-seal step inside the transaction that writes or deletes the salt and verifier, both of which live only in the database, so a failure rolls back to the old password with every row as it was. Erasing local data and database recovery delete the password and the sealed rows together.                                      |
| Connector permissions file and the vault key                                                                                                                         | Wire     | `connector-permissions.json` was sealed under the vault key when saved while unlocked. Locked, it read as empty, a saved block resolved to needs-approval, and the next save replaced every grant and block with one entry. The store decides which tools may run while locked, so it now lives under the machine key always; a vault-sealed file is moved back at every unlock and before a password change or removal commits. A store that exists and cannot be opened is never treated as empty: saving fails and changes nothing, and every tool resolves to blocked.                                                     |
| Resetting an unreadable connector permissions store                                                                                                                  | Wire     | Failing closed left no way out when no key opens the file. `connector_permission_list` returns the state of the store with the permissions, and `connector_permission_reset` renames an unreadable file beside itself with the time in its name, never deleting it, and writes an empty store. It does nothing when the store opens, and is refused while the vault is locked, since an unlock may bring the store back. A kept file that later opens under a vault key is merged back once at unlock, the stricter choice winning, and renamed `merged-`.                                                                     |
| Loosening a connector tool                                                                                                                                           | Wire     | `connector_permission_set` saved any level on the page's word, so a script could grant always allow or lift a block. Any change that makes a tool more permissive than its saved choice, or than the default the approval gate applies when nothing is saved, now goes through `sys::native_confirm` in a dialog naming the connector, the tool and what it does today. Tightening never asks. `tool_name_looks_destructive` is the one owner of the write-like test both approval gates use.                                                                                                                                  |
| A saved key and a page-supplied base URL                                                                                                                             | Wire     | `llm_configure_provider` bound the stored key to any base URL the page named. A saved key now goes only to the provider's registered endpoint (`default_base_url`); any other address needs the key in the same call or a native yes naming provider and address, remembered per pair and forgotten when the key is removed. The dialog is raised only for a call marked `userInitiated` (Reconnect or Save key on the Models row). Rehydration and every background path leave the provider unconnected, and its status row says "Reconnect to confirm sending your saved key to <host>".                                     |
| Setting or changing the master password                                                                                                                              | Wire     | Setup sealed every saved key under a password of the caller's choosing and change replaced it, both on the page's word, so a script could lock the user out of their keys. Both now take a native yes that says the keys will need the password at every start and that it cannot be recovered. The password is still typed in the window, and a password that breaks the rule is turned down before anything is asked.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `secret_manager_has`, `_set`, `_delete`                                                                                                                              | Wire     | Each took any key in the secret manager's `settings` table, so the page could probe, overwrite or delete the vault salt, the proxy profile or session secrets. They now accept one key, `perplexity_api_key`, the only one a live caller uses (Research settings). The three generic helpers in `securityStore.ts` had no caller and are cut.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `settings_v2_delete`                                                                                                                                                 | Cut      | It deleted any `settings_v2` row by key, so the page could remove saved keys, connector sign-ins or the approved-hooks list. Its one caller withdraws computer-use consent, which is now `computer_use_revoke_consent`: it takes no key and also ends grants already issued. No page-callable command reads or writes `settings_v2` by key; the page-writable allowlist is empty.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| BYOK dictation key                                                                                                                                                   | Wire     | `voice_transcribe_blob` and `speech_stop_and_transcribe` read `api_key_openai` through the settings service, which decrypts under a different key from the one `save_api_key` seals with, so BYOK dictation never found its key. Both now read it through `retrieve_api_key`, and a key sealed under a locked master password is reported as locked instead of missing. Either way the audio is sent nowhere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SettingsService::save_api_key`, `get_api_key`                                                                                                                       | Cut      | A second codec for `api_key_*` rows that no writer used and whose only readers were the two dictation commands above. Provider keys have one codec, `sys/security/vault_credentials.rs`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Stored Argon2 parameters                                                                                                                                             | Wire     | A version 3 row is verified with the parameters stored beside it, and a version 1 or 2 row with those in its PHC string, so a tampered row could ask for hours of work or all of memory. Both are checked against a floor and a ceiling before any hashing; a row outside them is refused with the numbers named.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Master password key derivation, version 3                                                                                                                            | Wire     | A version 2 row stored an Argon2 PHC string whose hash field was the vault key: verifier and key came from the same Argon2 call over the same salt, so reading the `master_password` row gave every purpose key. Version 3 runs Argon2id once to a root that is never stored and takes two HKDF-SHA256 branches with fixed, distinct labels: the verifier, stored and compared in constant time, and the vault key, held only in memory. HKDF is built on the `hmac` crate already present; no dependency was added. `setup_with` and `change` write version 3 only, and store the Argon2 parameters the row is verified with. |
| Version 1 and 2 master password rows                                                                                                                                 | Wire     | Read only to replace them. At the next successful unlock a new salt is drawn (the old one would reproduce the leaked key as the new root), everything the old keys sealed is re-sealed under the new key and the row is replaced, in one transaction, so a crash leaves the old row and all it opens intact. A failed upgrade is logged, the vault opens under the stored row, and the next unlock retries. A database copied while it held a version 2 row still gives up the keys sealed then; rotating those provider keys is the only remedy.                                                                              |
| Legacy vault keys by build date                                                                                                                                      | Wire     | Builds from 2026-01-30 to 2026-05-05 derived the vault key with two Argon2 iterations; builds from 2026-05-05 ran three for every older row whatever its PHC string recorded, so such a row can hold data under both keys and the upgrade tried only one. Every key a row could have used is now derived (the fixed three-iteration key, and the key at the cost the PHC string records) and tried in turn. An upgrade, password change or removal is refused while a saved provider key opens under none of them nor the device key: the row and every ciphertext stay as they were and the providers are named to the user.  |
| Sealing saved keys when a master password is set                                                                                                                     | Wire     | `needs_migration` counted rows in the old `settings` table while saved keys live in `settings_v2`, so keys saved before the password stayed under the per-install key, usable while locked, with no banner. `setup_with` now re-seals every vault-owned row in the transaction that stores the salt and verifier, and `needs_migration` is true exactly when a vault-owned `settings_v2` row still opens under the per-install key, locked or unlocked. A row no key opens is not counted, since no migration can fix it. Settings shows the banner while locked too.                                                          |
| `master_password_start_migration`, `_complete_migration`, `update_migration_progress`                                                                                | Cut      | They wrote the `master_password_migration` status row that used to decide `needs_migration`. That answer now comes from the rows themselves, so nothing read the status. The table stays in migration v51 and is no longer written.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Connector app credentials (`mcp_oauth_config_*`)                                                                                                                     | Cut      | Cut from the vault. A token refresh needs the client secret beside the refresh token and runs unattended while locked, the stated reason connector sign-ins stay under the per-install key; a locked vault made refresh fail with a "re-enter credentials" warning. They are saved under the per-install key again, and a row an earlier build sealed under the vault key is moved back at unlock, password change and removal; until then its readers say the vault is locked. Gating refresh on the vault was rejected: it stops scheduled runs to guard a secret stored beside its own refresh token.                       |
| Master password rule                                                                                                                                                 | Wire     | Settings stated and checked 8 characters; the native side requires 12 and three of four character classes, and reported the class rule as a "Cryptographic error". The numbers have one home, `PasswordPolicy::CURRENT`, sent with `master_password_get_status`; Settings states and checks them before submitting, and a refusal comes back as a typed `violation` that Settings words itself. The browser fixture in `tauri-mock.ts` carries the same two numbers, held to the Rust constants by a test.                                                                                                                     |
| BYOK providers after a restart with a master password                                                                                                                | Wire     | Keys encrypted under the master key cannot be read while the vault is locked, so every provider stayed unconfigured. `master_password_unlock` now restores them, and `MasterPasswordLaunchUnlock.tsx` asks for the password at launch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Managed-cloud branch of `chat_send_message`                                                                                                                          | Cut      | The renderer always sends Local or BYOK through Rust; managed chats use `CloudRuntime.ts`. The branch carried a hardcoded `free` plan tier and was the only caller of the monthly budget check.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Monthly budget                                                                                                                                                       | Wire     | It was checked only on the unreachable managed-cloud branch, so spend against the user's own keys was never bounded by it. `core/llm/daily_budget.rs` now owns it as a second window over the spend ledger, and `enforce_spend_caps` in `core/llm/llm_router.rs` applies it beside the daily cap to every request that is not to a Local runtime: chat, agents, Tasks and scheduled runs. The Usage dashboard draws its budget bar from the same figure.                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Native telemetry consent                                                                                                                                             | Wire     | `settings_update_privacy` had no caller and its insert violated the `settings_v2` category check, so the Rust collector was always denied. The analytics service now records consent there on change, at startup and on deletion.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `agent:cancelled`, `agent:paused`, `agent:resumed`                                                                                                                   | Cut      | Emitted with no listener. The Tasks panel follows `agi:goal:*`; orchestrator state reaches the renderer through `agent:status:update`; the only callers of orchestrator pause and resume update their own store.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Native canvas and A2UI: 13 `canvas_*` commands, `features/canvas`, `CanvasStateManager`, the `canvas:updated` event                                                  | Cut      | Wrapper-only: nothing in the renderer, the tool executor or the agent tools reached them, and the event had no listener. The renderer's `features/canvas/CanvasWorkspace.tsx` draws on its own and uses no IPC, so it stays.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

## 2026-10-11: Tauri chat and memory inventory

| Capability                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Floating quick chat                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Wire     | The window added an empty assistant message, invoked `chat_send_message` and discarded the result; the only stream listeners were inside `TauriRuntime`. It now sends through `TauriRuntime` and renders its stream, with Stop in the send slot. It is `local_only`: it uses the main window's model when that model runs on this device, never one behind a provider key, and says so instead of sending while the app is in Cloud mode.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Recent chats and the `conversation_search` and `recent_chats` model tools                                                                                                                                                                                   | Wire     | `get_recent_conversations` and `search_past_conversations` read every account's Local and Cloud conversations. Both now take a `ConversationScope` (account, execution boundaries); recents also skip archived chats. A model tool takes the one boundary of the conversation the turn runs in (`local_only`, `byok` or `cloud_managed`) and refuses when there is none, so a BYOK provider never reads a Local-only chat. The IPC command requires `userId` and `executionModes`; the person's own picker names both on-device boundaries. Neither tool could run before 2026-10-11 round 22: the tool guard had no policy for them and refuses a tool it does not know.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Sources on Local turns                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Wire     | `search_web` and project-knowledge retrieval emit `chat:sources`; `TauriRuntime` maps it to the canonical `source-list` event, so the activity row reads "Searched the web · N sources". `MessageBubble` lists the web sources of a turn with canonical activity once, as citation links under the answer; a turn without canonical activity keeps the result card as its only list. A knowledge file has no address and is counted, not linked. The searches are stored with the reply (`messages.sources`, migration v88) and a reopened chat restores them as the same citation links.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Folder memory                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Wire     | Nothing sent `projectFolder`, so the exact-folder branch of memory injection never ran and the folder the composer showed was not the folder the host used after a restart. `TauriRuntime` sends it. `ProjectSettingsDialog` lists, adds and deletes the folder's memories through `get_project_memories`, `save_architectural_decision` and `delete_project_memory`; delete asks first.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Other project memory IPC (10 commands) and `api/projectMemory.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                           | Cut      | No caller after the panel existed. The request structs took snake_case fields the renderer never sent, so the two store actions that invoked them could not have succeeded.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `~/.agiworkforce/INSTRUCTIONS.md`                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Wire     | Settings edited it and no chat read it. It is now the last section of the user instructions prompt, below this chat's and the saved instructions, and the prompt says repository instruction files override it: the CLI loads it root-first, before a project's files. The CLI memory file `raw_memories.md` stays CLI-only and the Settings copy now says so.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Artifact unarchive                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Wire     | Archive was one-way from the UI. The artifact menu offers Unarchive for an archived artifact.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Artifact streaming, rendered view, diff apply, tags, in-memory list, version diff, stats, export, import, clear (14 commands), `core/artifacts/renderer.rs`                                                                                                 | Cut      | Store actions with no caller; live drafts come from `chat:artifact-progress`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Memory Markdown export                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Wire     | Settings, Memory offers it beside the JSON backup. The `path` argument of both export commands is gone: no caller passed it, and it let the renderer name any file to write.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Memory IPC with no caller (13), chat memory IPC (7), decision detection                                                                                                                                                                                     | Cut      | `memory_remember`, `memory_recall`, `memory_search` and `memory_forget` shared their names with the model tools, which call the manager directly; the renderer never invoked them. `memory_get_decay_config` was first kept for the state bridge and cut later the same day (dead command sweep below): nothing called `initStateBridges`, so the bridge never ran. The manager methods only these commands reached are deleted with them, as is the never-sent `task_metadata` request field.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `chat_handle_stop`, `chat_get_pending_messages`, `chat_clear_pending_messages`, `project_get`, `project_get_settings`, config hierarchy (3 commands and `data/config_hierarchy.rs`)                                                                         | Cut      | Wrapper-only. `chat_stop_generation` is the stop path.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Request fields `strategy`, `workflow_hash`, `is_explicit_model_selection`; events `chat:context-compacted`, `chat:pending-context-available`                                                                                                                | Cut      | Never read; no listener.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

The request field stays because the MCP server bridge sets it in-process to keep skills out of external calls; it is no longer deserialized from the renderer.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `features/feedback`, `useGlobalSearch`, `api/chat.ts`, `lib/diffUtils.ts`, `lib/taskMetadata.ts`, `lib/intentClassifier.ts`, `lib/toolMatcher.ts`                                                                                                           | Cut      | Unreachable from `main.tsx`. The last three were held reachable only by a type import in a request interface nothing used. `submit_feedback`, `record_message_feedback` and `get_filtered_logs` went with them: message feedback is `chat_set_message_feedback`, and the send-feedback dialog was the only caller of the other two.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
