Page and phone cannot grant and get only the name.                                                                                                                                                               |
| App-server `settings/write`                                                                                                                                                                                    | Recorded, not built | Recommendation: wire only with a settings surface for the local agent's default model and permission mode. Until then the CLI's own configuration owns them.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| App-server `thread/handoff` and `thread/handoff/accept`                                                                                                                                                        | Recorded, not built | Recommendation: hold for a product decision. A handoff moves a session across a trust boundary and needs the explicit, labelled fork that boundary requires.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| App-server `account/token`                                                                                                                                                                                     | Recorded, not built | Recommendation: do not wire. The shell signs the CLI in by device code and has no use for the raw token.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Dropped notifications `server/warning` and `task/state_changed`                                                                                                                                                | Recorded, not built | Recommendation: show `server/warning` as a notice in the session panel, which is cheap; leave `task/state_changed` until background tasks have a surface.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Interactive terminal pane                                                                                                                                                                                      | Recorded, not built | The agent can keep a command running and drive it. `shell_start` runs it in a pseudo-terminal from `/usr/bin/script` on macOS and with pipes elsewhere; `shell_read` returns its output and types into it, with typed input approved like a command unless the shell policy allows that line; `shell_stop` ends it. The run is listed with the other work that keeps running and can be stopped there. Only a pane the user types into is not built. Recommendation: hold. A pane needs an approval model that a per-command prompt does not give; decide it before building one.                                                                                   |
| Agent approval to call an MCP tool, answered from the page or a paired phone                                                                                                                                   | Restrict            | `McpTool` left the routine kinds in `approvalReview.ts`. A tool server acts outside the sandbox with access this shell cannot see, so the allow is confirmed in a native dialog at the computer, as `Hook` and `Subagent` are; the phone is sent `approval-held` and told how it ended. Denying needs no dialog.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Agent approval whose kind this shell cannot read back                                                                                                                                                          | Restrict            | Admission and reading now key on one parsed name with one reader per routine kind. An `Exec`, file, push or network request whose command, paths, remote or tool is not in the shape expected is reserved (`unread-request`) rather than treated as reaching nothing. A network request is routine only from the sandboxed command tool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Environment the app hands the AGI CLI                                                                                                                                                                          | Restrict            | `runtimeEnvironment.ts` removes `AGIWORKFORCE_NO_SANDBOX`, `AGIWORKFORCE_SANDBOX_HOOKS`, `AGIWORKFORCE_SANDBOX_MCP`, `AGI_POWERSHELL_ALLOW_UNSAFE` and `AGI_A2A_ALLOW_PRIVATE` before `agi app-server` or `agi logout` starts, so an app launched from a shell that set one does not run sessions unsandboxed. A test holds each name to the CLI source.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Runtime command no table names                                                                                                                                                                                 | Restrict            | `dispatch` ran any command with a `case` and no capability entry. It now refuses a command unless exactly one of the folder table, the session table or the explicit `UNGATED_COMMANDS` set names it, and a test reads the `case` labels from the source to hold that.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Stop Agents shortcut set from the settings page                                                                                                                                                                | Restrict            | The page could clear the chord, and hide the menu bar item, with no prompt, leaving no stop that works from another app while an agent holds the pointer. The shell refuses a write that sets it to none or to a chord it does not offer, reads a stored empty value as the default, and lets it keep its chord when another shortcut asks for the same one. The panel still lists "No shortcut" for it and shows the refusal; removing that option is a web change.                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Sandbox profile for local commands                                                                                                                                                                             | Restrict            | `shellSandbox.ts` now follows the CLI's Seatbelt write rules in its order. Protected entries are denied by name at any depth, in any case, hook managers' files included; allowed again inside a dependency or build tree; denied at the folder's top. Creating `.vscode` is denied, a protected path that is a link is denied as link and target, and the 34 credential locations are unreadable even inside a readable toolchain folder. A hook manager's install under the sandbox now fails.                                                                                                                                                                    |
| What a sandboxed local command may read and reach                                                                                                                                                              | Restrict            | A PATH entry's parent was readable, so `~/Library/pnpm` opened all of `~/Library`. The readable folder is now the entry itself, or its parent only for `bin` and `sbin` outside the home folder's wide folders; the app's own data and the CLI's folders are unreadable. The network checkbox opens hosts elsewhere only: local ports and Unix sockets stay closed. Submodule gitfiles are also read from the git index, which a command cannot rewrite.                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Shortcuts set from the settings page                                                                                                                                                                           | Restrict            | Quick Ask, Screenshot and Dictation took any string from the page and registered it for every app, so Screenshot on the paste chord captured the screen at each paste. Every shortcut now takes only a chord the shell offers for it, the one it has, or none where none is allowed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Native approval dialogs                                                                                                                                                                                        | Restrict            | Both dialogs say what the app knows first (folder, kind, risk, sandbox, network, what allowing does), then a line that nothing below is the app speaking, then each part of the request on one labelled line: a line break or tab in a value is drawn as a mark, so an argument cannot forge a `Sandbox:` line. A Cancel is kept per request for the page and for the phone, and the two share one open dialog.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Protected and credential names spelled with characters the volume folds                                                                                                                                        | Restrict            | APFS opens `.mcp.json` for `.mcp.jſon` and `.ssh` for `.ẞh`; lower case alone matched neither, so no dialog was raised. Names are compared after `foldedFileName` (NFKC, case mapping repeated until it settles), which covers the eleven code points a probe of the volume found. The Rust list still lowers case only.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Text a page, an agent or a remote peer supplies to a native dialog or notification                                                                                                                             | Restrict            | One module, `runtime/nativeSurface.ts`, calls `dialog.showMessageBox` and `new Notification`. Every call site goes through it and its text passes `visibleInDialog`, so a line break, tab or hidden character in a command, name or path is drawn as a mark. The shell-run prompt states the app's own facts first. A source-reading test fails a second caller.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Home-folder tool directories a sandboxed local command may read                                                                                                                                                | Restrict            | A `bin` on PATH inside the home folder opened its parent, so `~/.local/bin` made `~/.local/share` readable. Inside home only the entry and its sibling `lib` and `libexec` are read; outside home the parent still is, unless it is top-level.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Name lookups and Apple developer tools inside the sandbox                                                                                                                                                      | Wire                | `/usr/bin/git` is a shim that needs the developer directory, and resolving a name needs the metadata of `/var`. The profile reads the directory `DEVELOPER_DIR` or the default names, and the `/var` link; live tests run `git --version` and a lookup under `sandbox-exec`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Folders holding a nested checkout inside a writable root                                                                                                                                                       | Restrict            | A command could rename a submodule folder aside and put its own in its place, taking the protected `.git` entry with it. The profile denies create and unlink on each such folder and on each folder above it below the root; a live test fails to swap one.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Stop Agents chord the system will not register, and the tray icon while agents can act                                                                                                                         | Restrict            | A settings write whose always-on chord does not register is rolled back and reported, not saved as if it worked. The tray icon stays while computer use is granted or an agent turn runs, whatever the hide setting says: it is then the remaining way to stop.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Browser pairing over the loopback bridge                                                                                                                                                                       | Restrict            | Pair routes take the extension only from a `chrome-extension://` Origin equal to the id in the body, so a page, a sandboxed frame or a script cannot open or confirm one. The code is shown in a native dialog and is not in the state a page reads. The wildcard CORS header is gone, tokens are checked before a body is read, and a body is refused by its declared length.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Text file write from the page                                                                                                                                                                                  | Wire                | A write emptied the file and then filled it, so a full disk or a crash lost what was there. The contents go to a new file beside it and are synced; the name is checked again and only then moved onto them. Permission bits are kept. A hard-linked file is still refused, since the rename would leave its other name on the old contents.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Work stopped when a folder or its agent permission is withdrawn                                                                                                                                                | Restrict            | The commands to stop were read before the question was asked, so one started while it was on screen kept running after the grant went. They are read again after the answer.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Paired phone on remote control before it has signed anything                                                                                                                                                   | Restrict            | Who the peer is came from unsigned relay metadata, and the session list was sent before the phone signed anything. Nothing is sent, and no phone is remembered, until a control verifies under the scanned secret. A phone taken back keeps the name it was allowed by, and one nonce record per salt refuses a replay after a reconnect.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Folder dropped on the window, and the host an MCP server connects to                                                                                                                                           | Restrict            | The drop prompt shows the folder the grant will cover once links are resolved, and says so when what was dropped is a link. The MCP review shows the host of a URL in its ASCII form.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Credential locations a sandboxed local command cannot read                                                                                                                                                     | Restrict            | The list follows the crate's `CREDENTIAL_PATHS`, now 109 entries: 60 more files where command-line tools keep a login (forges, hosting and database platforms, secret managers, package managers, infrastructure tools, tunnels, model hubs) and the desktop chat apps' data. A parity test reads the crate's list entry for entry.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Agent approval that writes a file, judged where the write lands                                                                                                                                                | Restrict            | The path was judged after its `..` were taken out as text, so `lnk/../config` read as `config` while `lnk` linked into `.git/info` and the write went to `.git/config`: no native dialog. It is now also judged as written, links followed first. A direct write from the page already refused any `..`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| MCP server review: variables not passed, and launches nothing pins                                                                                                                                             | Restrict            | A variable the entry sets and the CLI will not hand the server is listed apart, as set in the config but not passed, never with a value. When the CLI marks part of a launch as not checked, the dialog no longer says the answer is remembered: it says the CLI asks at every start and that what runs can change between starts.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| File read, folder listing and open-with-default from the page                                                                                                                                                  | Restrict            | A read opened by path after its check, and a command renaming inside the folder made it return an outside file 493 times in six seconds. Reads and listings now open without following a link, verify what they opened and read from that handle. Open-with-default takes only inert document types by an allowlist; `.terminal`, `.webloc` and the rest are revealed, not opened.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Computer use: a native yes for each run                                                                                                                                                                        | Restrict            | One session grant served every screen step to any script on the page's origin. Each run, named by the id the page sends with its steps, now takes its own yes in main, kept for five idle minutes at most; a step that names no run is refused and a refused run stays refused.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Agent approvals a page or phone may answer alone                                                                                                                                                               | Restrict            | An edit to a file inside the approved folder that is not git metadata, project configuration or a hook manager's file, at low or medium risk, and the kinds that move nothing outside the conversation. Every command, network lift and push, anything high risk or unrated, and any write outside the folder or to a protected path is confirmed in a native dialog. The system's own banner may still approve a command the CLI says changes nothing.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Native questions a page can raise repeatedly                                                                                                                                                                   | Restrict            | One question is open per subject, at most three wait at once, and for commands, screen runs, Bypass turns, allowed-program additions and preference changes a Cancel is remembered for a minute and three Cancels quiet that kind. Adding an allowed program needs local commands allowed in some folder first.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Deleting a coding session                                                                                                                                                                                      | Restrict            | The live agent grant was enough. Main now asks, naming the session by its title, with Cancel as the default, and says the conversation and checkpoints cannot be brought back.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Folders that can be approved as a workspace                                                                                                                                                                    | Restrict            | `/Users`, `/private`, `~/Library`, `~/.ssh` and the home folder by its data-volume path were grantable. System folders, the home folder and anything above it by any spelling, and a folder that is, holds or sits inside a credential location are refused, and a stored grant that fails is dropped on load.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Launch at login, and clearing the chosen AGI CLI, from the page                                                                                                                                                | Restrict            | Both changed without a question. Turning launch at login on, and going back to the built-in CLI, are asked in main; turning launch at login off and hiding the menu bar icon are not, and the icon stays while an agent can act.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Permission prompt answers, AGI CLI spawn, submodule map, MCP review wording, blank runs in dialogs                                                                                                             | Restrict            | A prompt grants only on its approving buttons. The CLI is spawned by absolute path, never a bare name. A page cannot write `.gitmodules`. The MCP review says a launch is asked about at every start only from the CLI's own field, never from a file name. Runs of blanks and invisible fillers in a dialog value are drawn as a count or a mark.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| A queued or steered follow-up whose send was not accepted (shared chat)                                                                                                                                        | Wire                | `sendFollowUpById` removed the follow-up before `startTurn`, which can turn a send away after a toast, so the text vanished. `startTurn` now reports whether the turn started; a follow-up that did not start stays, marked Not sent with the reason, and is never sent on its own. A steer the host refused outright and a steer a runtime cannot take back are kept the same way. Regenerate, retry and edit put the transcript back when their turn does not start. The shared composer clears what was typed and attached only when its host reports the send was taken (`ComposerSendOutcome`); a send turned away stays in the box with the reason beside it. |
| Local session follow-ups, held message and draft across Back or another session                                                                                                                                | Wire                | They lived in the panel, which is mounted per session. They now live in `local-session-memory.ts`, by folder and thread, are there when the session is opened again, wait for the user if no turn is running to send them, and are dropped when the thread is deleted or its worktree removed. Attached pictures are not kept.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Reference to a removed worktree's work                                                                                                                                                                         | Wire                | Removing a worktree closed the panel that would have shown the ref. The ref is handed to the page, which shows it on the session list the user lands on.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Local composer: IME confirm, repeated Enter, Retry                                                                                                                                                             | Wire                | Enter that confirms an IME word no longer sends. One press is one send while pictures are read, and a send made while a turn is starting or running is queued instead of replacing the live turn, so Stop keeps its turn id. Send it again takes the prompt from the stored conversation for a turn this page did not start, and is not offered when there is none.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Models for a folder whose refresh failed                                                                                                                                                                       | Wire                | One failed read dropped every other folder's models. The map is merged; a folder whose read failed keeps its last models and the session says they may be out of date.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

## 2026-10-11: Tauri cloud, trust boundary and routing inventory

Retained Tauri code only; nothing here changes the public Electron Desktop.

| Capability                                                          | Decision | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Native SQLite to cloud delta sync (chat, memory, projects)          | Cut      | `sync_conversations_to_cloud` refused every call unless `chat_storage_mode` was `cloud`, and nothing could set it, so the 30 s renderer trigger only logged a rejection. `data/cloud_sync.rs`, `memory_sync.rs`, `projects_sync.rs`, the trigger, the setting, the `mark_*_for_push` hooks and the `cloud_sync_enabled` flag through the send path are removed. Managed chats persist through `CloudRuntime` and the web API. The `cloud_id` columns from applied migrations stay. |
| Completion IPC (code, inline, prompt) and `promptCompletionEnabled` | Cut      | No caller reached `get_code_completion`, `get_inline_completion` or `get_prompt_completion`; the prompt path also added Managed Cloud and Zhipu candidates after the trust filter. The commands, the hook, the client wrappers and the setting, including its managed settings sync key, are removed.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Offline message queue                                               | Cut      | Nothing queued a message and no sync callback could send one. The queue and its sync manager are removed from Desktop; the shared package keeps them for Web. The offline banner now reads `appModeStore.isOnline`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `sys/diagnostics`                                                   | Cut      | 2,453 lines with no command and no caller; its network check would have contacted the API host in any mode. Startup recovery keeps its own sanitized export.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Project default privacy mode                                        | Cut      | The choice was saved and never read when a conversation was created. A new conversation is Local, and BYOK is reached only through the fork dialog, so the control and the stored field are removed. Cloud projects keep their `managed` label.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Process-wide app mode                                               | Wire     | Rust held no mode, so each cloud caller trusted its caller. `sys/account/app_mode.rs` holds Local or Cloud, defaults to Local, and is set by `account_set_app_mode` at startup and on every change. The account credential is readable only in Cloud, which covers the managed provider, media, transcription and the helper LLM command. Sign-in passes its own token and needs no exemption.                                                                                     |
| Managed Cloud registration                                          | Wire     | The provider was registered for any signed-in session and never removed. It is now registered only for a signed-in Cloud session and removed on leaving Cloud or signing out.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Dictation cleanup and provider                                      | Wire     | Cleanup sent no trust mode and a hosted default provider. It now sends the app's mode, uses only the selected local runtime in Local, and otherwise keeps the dictation as spoken with a note. A legacy `deepgram` choice maps to Local Whisper, and managed transcription is refused outside Cloud.                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Offline in Cloud                                                    | Wire     | The composer keeps its draft, disables send and says "You're offline" until the connection returns.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| AGI `llm_reason` provider and code analysis                         | Wire     | A provider name is resolved inside the goal's trust boundary, and an override outside it is dropped so routing stays inside. The code-analysis step no longer pins Anthropic.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Model name heuristics                                               | Wire     | The managed provider's Anthropic request shape and the web-search server tool are decided by the catalog entry, not by the model's name.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Tools withheld for a model                                          | Wire     | A turn that asked for tools and got none because of the model says so once per conversation and model, through the chat notice channel.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

## 2026-10-11: Tauri settings, notifications, browser and platform inventory

Retained Tauri code only. Checked by reading the code and by unit tests; no Rust was compiled and nothing here was exercised in a running app.

| Capability                                                                                                                                           | Decision    | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Notification settings and the notification list                                                                                                      | Wire        | Both lived in process memory. `sys/commands/notification_center.rs` now keeps the settings and the 200 most recent items in `settings_v2` (`notification_center.settings`, `notification_center.items`) and `lib.rs` restores them at startup. The wire format is camelCase, which is what `notificationStore.ts` already read; the snake_case copy of the types went with `useNotifications`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Notification click routing                                                                                                                           | Wire        | Emitters set `action_url` through `NotificationRoute` (trigger id, scheduler job id, background agent id, settings tab). `features/notifications/notificationRoutes.ts` opens the panel and `notificationTarget.ts` scrolls to, focuses and highlights that row in Automations, Scheduled or the Background tab once the list has loaded. The startup-recovery notice carries no route: nothing in the app shows degraded services. The autonomous-loop notices open Agent tasks without an item: that loop has no agent id and no caller. The installed `tauri-plugin-notification` 2.4.0 has no click callback on desktop, so an OS notification only brings the app forward.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Do-not-disturb                                                                                                                                       | Wire        | A notification raised during do-not-disturb was refused before it was stored, so a failed scheduled task was lost, not silenced. The rule in `deliver_notification`: the master switch or a type turned off refuses it and nothing is stored; otherwise it is stored, listed and counted on the badge, and do-not-disturb, the desktop switch and the sound switch only decide whether it interrupts.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Background-agent notices                                                                                                                             | Wire        | `core/agent/background_agent.rs` raised its finished and failed notices straight on the OS plugin, and the renderer raised a second one. Both are replaced by one `deliver_notification` call in the run loop, with the agent's route. `notification_create` is cut with its registration and mock: it had no caller left. `notification_center.rs` is the only file that calls the plugin, the `notification:allow-*` grants are gone from `capabilities/default.json`, and so are the two `@tauri-apps/plugin-notification` alias shims.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `notification_show`, the scheduled-notification and reminder commands, the two permission commands                                                   | Cut         | `sys/commands/notifications.rs` deleted with its state, wrappers, store actions and mock cases. No caller; scheduled reminders already exist as the scheduler's Notification action, and the desktop plugin reports permission as always granted.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Second notification store and second updater store in `windowStore.ts`; `useNotifications`                                                           | Cut         | No importer. The updater copy persisted a different shape under the same `agiworkforce-updater` key as `updaterStore.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `FontSelector`                                                                                                                                       | Cut         | No importer. `chatFont` stays: managed settings sync carries the web font choice through it, though the `--chat-font-family` variable it sets has no reader. Recommendation: read the variable in the transcript or stop syncing the field.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| AGI Code instruction files                                                                                                                           | Wire        | The tab edited seven files in the home directory that no prompt reads. It now edits `~/.agiworkforce/INSTRUCTIONS.md` through `dotfile_*_instructions` and the open project's `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md` and `.agi/instructions.md`, the files `chat/prompt_context.rs` loads, and says the other four names are not read.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Research mode, max sources, citations                                                                                                                | Cut         | Persisted and never read. The orchestrator takes its source count from the mode chosen on the research page and no report code reads a citation flag, so wiring them is not small. The controls are removed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Perplexity key                                                                                                                                       | Wire (copy) | The key is read by web search in chat (`tool_executor/search_tools.rs`) and in Tasks (`search_executor.rs`), not by Deep Research (`core/research/agents.rs` calls `run_search` without an app handle). The card says so. Removing the key asks first.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Terminal sandbox switch                                                                                                                              | Wire        | `terminal_sandbox_availability` runs the same `srt` lookup the launch uses. The switch refuses to turn on while the runtime is missing, shows the reason and offers the executable path; it is hidden on Windows, which has no backend.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Phone pairing                                                                                                                                        | Wire        | Connections is a section of `DesktopCloudSettingsModal` and Cowork's Manage pairing opens it. Connections and Cowork are hidden in the Local settings panel, where the managed boundary refuses pairing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Swarm run                                                                                                                                            | Wire        | `agi_submit_goal_swarm` spawns the run on a clone of the engine and returns the goal id, so status, list and cancel no longer wait on it. `core/swarm` tracks the run by goal id; `agi_cancel_goal` stops it, each sub-agent cancels its sub-goal, and the task ends Cancelled. The Tasks store shows `swarm:decomposed` and `swarm:subtask_*` as live steps. `swarm:started` and `swarm:completed` duplicated `agi:goal:swarm_*` and are removed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Swarm sub-goal events                                                                                                                                | Wire        | Each subtask ran as a goal whose state events added blank rows to the Tasks list, under ids such as `subtask_1` that repeat between runs. `agent_spawner.rs` now namespaces the sub-goal id under its parent (`swarm-subgoal-<goal>--<subtask>`) and `agentTaskStore.ts` ignores events for those ids; the parent row shows the subtasks as live steps.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Extension bridge token and port                                                                                                                      | Wire        | `integrations/native_messaging/endpoint.rs` owns the token path and the port. The Windows host read Local AppData while the app wrote Roaming; both now use `dirs::data_dir()`. The `AGI_REALTIME_PORT` override is dropped, since neither reader honoured it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `code_execute` on Windows                                                                                                                            | Wire        | `tool_executor/platform.rs` withholds it from every tool list where no network-denying sandbox exists and refuses a call with that reason.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Automation permissions on Windows and Linux                                                                                                          | Wire        | `check_automation_permissions` reports `support` per capability and the missing window-control program. The panel shows "No permission needed on this platform" or "Not supported on this platform" instead of a grant, and names `wmctrl` when Linux lacks it. `request_automation_permission` returns an error off macOS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Computer-use run-loop pointer steps on Windows and Linux                                                                                             | Wire        | A click, drag or scroll is sent only when the point is known to belong to the app the step was planned for. Windows now answers through `WindowFromPoint`, `GetAncestor` and `GetWindowThreadProcessId` in `automation/computer_use/target_app.rs`, and the front app carries its real process id. Linux stays refused: no owner query holds under both X11 and Wayland, so the loop is keyboard-only there. Uncompiled and unrun; tracked as `DESKTOP-COMPUTER-USE-POINTER-HIT-TEST-01`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Deep links on the Tauri scheme                                                                                                                       | Wire        | `desktopDeepLinkRouter.ts` maps `agiworkforce:` onto the contract scheme before parsing, so the same targets and id checks apply. Links only navigate.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Firefox and WebKit browser types, `PlaywrightBridge::start_server` and `stop_server`, `automation/browser/session.rs`, the seven `design_*` commands | Cut         | WebKit returned an error and Firefox was started with Chromium arguments; `start_server` returned "not implemented"; the other two had no caller.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Capability switches ("Tools the assistant may use")                                                                                                  | Wire        | The store held `features` and the backend refused a tool whose capability was off, but `setFeature` had no caller, so every capability was always on. `capabilities.rs` now owns the ten names with their labels and descriptions (`CAPABILITIES`), `list_capabilities` returns them with the default and tool count, and `set_capability` saves one switch to `settings.json` before enforcing it. Settings, Capabilities renders the rows from that command through `CapabilityToggles.tsx`. The backend reads the switches from `settings.json` in setup, ahead of the scheduler, the background agents and the triggers, so a run at launch is held to them. A missing file means defaults. A file or switches that cannot be read hold every capability that acts on the computer off (all but screenshots) until the person turns one on or the file is read in full; a renderer sync does not release them, and the section and the refusal say why. `list_capabilities` reads the registry once. A second change the same day stopped a disabled capability's tools being offered to the model and put the switches in front of every agent step, background agent and goal run (`CapabilityState::tool_refusal`). Not covered, and the section says so: shell commands and scripts the person schedules themselves. |
| Unreadable `settings.json`                                                                                                                           | Wire        | After a failed read, a switch or Save rewrote the file from in-memory defaults and whatever could have been recovered was gone. The one writer (`persist_settings_snapshot_to_path` in `sys/commands/settings.rs`) now renames a file that does not parse as settings to `settings.json.unreadable-<UTC time>` beside it before the staged write. It never deletes it, a failed rename stops the write, and a readable, missing or empty file is left alone, so one damaged file is kept once. Settings names the kept file for the rest of the session (`settings_kept_unreadable_file`, `KeptSettingsFileNotice.tsx`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Who may change a capability switch                                                                                                                   | Wire        | The page could turn every switch on: `sync_capabilities` replaced the whole map and `set_capability(name, true)` asked nothing, and this webview renders model output. `sync_capabilities` is cut with its four callers in `settingsStore.ts`; `settings_save` keeps the switches the backend holds and drops what the page sent; `settings_load_from_disk` no longer changes them. `set_capability` is the only writer: off is free, on is decided in `sys::native_confirm` with the capability named and what it allows.
