The section shows the native step.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Settings and switches before the renderer                                                                                                            | Wire        | `SettingsState` was defaults until the page loaded the file, so the first toggle or folder grant rewrote `settings.json` from defaults. `read_startup_settings` reads the file once in setup, ahead of the scheduler, background agents and triggers, and feeds both `SettingsState` and `CapabilityState`. A document that fails to parse still yields its switches when those parse alone. When they do not, each acting capability is held off and recorded `false` in the settings the app holds, so the file that replaces the unreadable one says off and the next launch starts off; a hold ends only when the person turns that capability on. Hooks startup moved ahead of the same runners.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| What a capability switch promises                                                                                                                    | Wire (copy) | Each `Off:` sentence in `CAPABILITIES` now says which built-in tools are withheld and a test holds that wording. The section says tools from MCP servers and connectors are not governed by these switches, apart from the built-in file server, and links to Settings, Connectors, where each server is switched.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Device automation panels outside Local mode                                                                                                          | Wire        | Triggers, the scheduler and background agents start in every mode, but Automations, Scheduled and Agent tasks were Local-only panels, so their notices opened nothing elsewhere. `v3/shellPanels.ts` owns panel availability; the three panels open in every mode with a line saying they run on this device, and the cloud Tasks and Schedules panels link to them.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Native reasons reach the person                                                                                                                      | Wire        | `getSimpleErrorMessage` in `src/lib/errorMessages.ts` turned every message it did not recognise into one generic sentence and rewrote any message containing `api key`, `network`, `500` or `timeout`, so a refusal a command wrote for the person never arrived. It now shows what the command said, its first two lines, bounded, control characters removed, read from a string, an `Error` or a serialised `AGIError` (`details`, or the nested `message`). Transport and provider failures are still reworded, by shape: a status standing as a status, `llm_error_type`, a provider error code, the browser's fetch wording as the whole message. A status that carries the server's own detail keeps it after our sentence (`The model server reported an error: model requires more memory`). The generic sentence is left for an error with no words and for the runtime's own wording. The scheduler's `taskErrorMessage` bypass is removed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Terminal panel                                                                                                                                       | Wire        | The panel is the person's own shell, so nothing typed there is filtered: the keystroke check that passed any command a byte at a time is gone from `terminal_send_input`, and the window's substring `confirm` with it. The first terminal of an app run is decided in `sys::native_confirm`, naming the folder; every other session command refuses until then. `TerminalWorkspace.tsx` shows the host's refusal, asks once and claims nothing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| General shell command for the page                                                                                                                   | Cut         | `execute_terminal_command` ran any command line for the page. Its only callers ran `claude --version` and `claude plugin(s) install, update, remove`, now `claude_plugin_cli_available` and `claude_plugin_change` in `sys/commands/claude_plugins.rs`: fixed program, validated plugin name, no shell, native yes before install or update. The function stays for the app's own MCP server; it is no longer registered.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Network proxy and root certificate                                                                                                                   | Wire        | `llm_network_proxy_set` let the page route every provider call through a proxy and trust a certificate it wrote itself. A profile that routes or trusts, and is not the one approved, is decided in a native dialog naming the proxy host and the certificate file with its SHA-256; a file inside the app's own folders is refused; the approved hash is kept and checked at startup; a saved password stays with the scheme, host and port it was saved for. Turning both off never asks.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| What a settings save may loosen                                                                                                                      | Wire        | `settings_save` took folder grants and the terminal sandbox from the page. A new folder must be one the native picker returned (`sys::picked_folders`), also in `project_context_set_folder`. Sandbox off, full access, no backend, read-only to workspace-write, another sandbox program, an added domain, or approving unanswered tool calls on timeout is decided in a native dialog naming the value now and after; a no saves nothing. Tightening never asks.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

The entries were saved to `customModels` and nothing offered them as a model. Card, store fields, endpoint validator and shared type are gone; the key is no longer written and an older file still parses. `alwaysUseAgentMode`, stored with no control and no reader, is dropped the same way.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Database commands for the page                                                                                                                       | Cut         | Forty `db_*` commands opened connections and ran queries for the page with no approval, behind `databaseStore.ts`, which no screen mounted. Commands, store and wrapper are gone; `DatabaseState` stays for the assistant's tools. Those tools now have no way to get a connection, as before: `DESKTOP-DATABASE-TOOLS-NO-CONNECTION-01`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

Each now passes `file_ops::require_granted_path`, the test `file_read` applies. ## 2026-10-11: dead command sweep

170 of 834 registrations removed: 154 commands deleted and 16 deregistered because Rust still calls the function in process. A follow-up the same day removed 38 more (34 deleted, 4 deregistered) and registered `cloud_disconnect`. 627 remain.

| Capability                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Decision    | Evidence and retained path -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Stricter wiring guard                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Wire        | `apps/desktop/scripts/check-wiring.mjs` counted any `command('x')` in `packages/client/desktop-command-client` as a caller, so a wrapper nobody imported kept its command alive. A wrapper now counts only when a module reachable from `src/main.tsx` references its export by name, through a namespace member, a re-export, a destructure or an awaited dynamic import; a namespace passed on as a value keeps all of its wrappers. The first run reported 165 commands. Type imports, shadowing locals and unreachable importers do not count. The follow-up applies the same rule to every reachable module that calls `invoke` directly, which reported 38 more commands, and adds a `DEAD CALLER` group for a function nobody references even when its commands are live elsewhere (350 on its first run: 331 wrappers, 19 renderer functions). A later pass follows default imports instead of treating every default export as used, and reports a class or object literal nobody references together with every command only it reaches. Not covered: a single unused method inside a class or object literal that is itself used, and a function in a module `main.tsx` never reaches, which is left to the module reachability baseline. | Session IPC (3): `auth_store_session`, `auth_retrieve_session`, `auth_remove_session`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Cut         | No caller. `validate_jwt` went with them. The follow-up deleted `SessionState` and `get_session_user_id`: nothing wrote the session, so every reader saw `default`. Changed signatures: `track_workflow_view(workflow_id, state)` takes its user id from the telemetry collector (`stores/billing/analyticsSlice.ts` through `api/analytics.ts`); `mcp_set_credential(server_name, key, value)` and `mcp_delete_credential(server_name, key)` lose the session parameter and with it a `user_id == "default"` check that refused every call (`McpClient.setCredential` and `deleteCredential` in `api/mcp.ts`). Both now take `McpState`. `mcp_set_credential` refuses a server that is not configured or a key that is not one of its `<from_credential_manager>` slots, the same list `mcp_list_credential_slots` shows. `mcp_delete_credential` accepts the same, and also any server and key that still has a stored value, so a stored secret can always be deleted. Removing a server (`mcp_update_config`, `mcpb_uninstall_bundle`, `mcp_oauth_disconnect`) deletes its stored credential rows in the same command, except a row another configured server claims; a config reload does not, because the active config changes with the project. No command returns a stored value. Rows are named `mcp_secret/<scope>/<hex of the server name>/<slot>`, so no two servers share one: the old `mcp_credential_<server>_<slot>` let `github_GITHUB_PERSONAL` with slot `ACCESS_TOKEN` read `github`'s `GITHUB_PERSONAL_ACCESS_TOKEN`. The scope is `user`, or the hash of the project folder whose `.mcp.json` is active, so a project server, which reaches the resolver once the user enables it, reads only what was stored for that folder. An old row is moved to its new name on first use, in the user scope only, and only when exactly one configured server and slot spell it. Project consent now pins a `<from_…>` reference, so a consented project server cannot be re-pointed at another stored credential without consent again. The other four readers were commands with no caller and are cut below.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

| `privacy_export_data`, `privacy_delete_account`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Wire        | Settings, Privacy now calls both. Export: `privacy_export_data(app, state) -> Option<String>` opens the native save dialog itself, writes every user table with credential columns redacted through the no-follow writer, and returns the file's name; neither the path nor the content passes through the renderer. Delete: `privacy_delete_account(state, secret_state, app_handle) -> bool` no longer takes a user id and asks first in a dialog the operating system draws (`sys::native_confirm`, consenting button "Delete All Data"), so nothing running in the window can answer it; `false` means declined, nothing changed. It removes the Cloud sign-in from the OS keyring, the files its rows point at inside the app's own folders (before the rows, so a stopped run can be run again), every row of every user table, clears web data through the webview, and leaves a marker. The marker is a claim, not proof: at the yes the command puts 32 random bytes in the OS credential store (service the bundle id, account `pending-local-wipe`, an item of its own beside the install secret) before anything is deleted, and writes the same value into the marker afterwards. When the credential store refuses, the command says nothing was deleted and schedules nothing; a deletion that fails takes its proof back. At the next launch `finish_pending_local_wipe_before_webview` runs between `build` and `run`, after the single-instance check and before any webview exists, and obeys the marker only when it equals the stored proof (constant-time, 64 hex characters). Any other marker is removed and logged, never obeyed; when the store cannot be read the marker waits and nothing is wiped. A proof is good for one launch: every launch that reaches the store leaves no proof in it, marker or not, so a copied or withheld marker cannot be put back later. That is one credential-store delete call at each launch. A marker that matches empties the app data, local data and cache folders (on Windows and Linux the local data folder is also the webview's data folder) and on macOS removes `~/Library/WebKit/<bundle id>`, `~/Library/HTTPStorages/<bundle id>` and `~/Library/Cookies/<bundle id>.binarycookies`, the bundle id read from the app config; a link standing in for one is left alone. It also empties the log folder, which sits outside the app's folders because logging starts first, and removes the screen captures, screenshots and browser profiles kept under the shared `agiworkforce` data folder, and `~/.agiworkforce/security-audit.log`, the app's own log of every terminal command it ran (`sys/security/command_validator.rs`); that one file, the folder stays. The renderer asks through `useConfirmAction`, then signs out, clears its storage and relaunches. The restart does not depend on it: after a confirmed deletion Rust calls `request_restart` itself once 5 seconds have passed (`RESTART_AFTER_DELETION`, the time the window has to end the Cloud session on the server), and a restart cannot be held by the quit question (`sys::shutdown::handle_exit_requested`), so the request and its proof are open for seconds, not until the user happens to restart. Not removed: the database key and install secret in the keyring, installed extensions and skills under the OS data and config folders, and the rest of `~/.agiworkforce`. |
| `export_user_data`, `clear_local_database`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Cut         | Superseded. The first exported conversations, messages and unencrypted settings only; the second cleared eight tables for the old Clear All Data button, which Delete all data on this device replaces. Their command-client wrappers went with them, leaving the `chat` and `cache` modules empty and deleted.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Pending account deletion: `privacy_request_account_deletion`, `privacy_get_pending_deletion`, `privacy_cancel_pending_deletion`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Cut         | No caller and no command attribute. They wrote a `pending_deletion.json` marker with a seven-day grace date and nothing ever acted on it. Deleting what is on the device is `privacy_delete_account`, and deleting a Cloud account belongs to the Cloud side (Manage Cloud privacy). `PendingDeletionStatus`, the marker record and the two constants went with them.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| What the local export holds                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Wire        | `privacy_export_data` classifies every table by name in `sys/commands/privacy.rs`: 96 exported, 2 settings stores exported without their sealed rows and without the `security` category of `settings_v2`, 8 never exported because every row is a secret, credential or verifier (`master_password`, `api_keys`, `auth_sessions`, `oauth_providers`, `browser_sessions`, `messaging_connections`, `calendar_accounts`, `email_accounts`), 22 internal. A table nobody classified is left out and named in the export metadata, and a test fails until it is classified. Secret-looking names are still redacted inside what is exported, including inside stored JSON, compared without case or hyphens. The `security` category is left out because, unsealed, it records what the app has been allowed to do, not what the user wrote: `hooks_approved_yaml` (every approved hook command and its environment) and `hooks_approval_notified_fingerprint`, `mcp_project_consent_*`, `skill_project_consent_*`, `mcp_unsandboxed_*`, the unsealed `mcp_oauth_*` bookkeeping, `tool_confirmation.agent_mode`, `tool_confirmation.auto_approve_all` and `computer_use.consent`. A string that is one address and nothing else, under any name or none, in a row or a stored document, is reduced by the launch policy to scheme, host, port and path with ` (credentials hidden)` when it had a user, password, query or fragment, and hidden whole when the policy cannot read it; an address inside a sentence is not touched. A map of variables or headers is found by its name or its last word, in any case style (`env`, `envs`, `environment`, `vars`, `headers`, so `http_headers`, `requestHeaders`, `env_vars`). Every value in one is hidden unless the launch policy's `shown_value` shows it, the arguments of a launch command are hidden whole with only their number kept, and a command line held as one string (`command`, `cmd`, `command_line`, `script`) keeps its program and hides the rest. The export also carries `scheduler.db`, `knowledge.db`, `app_permissions.json`, `trusted_workflows.json` and the two files the app shares with the command-line tool in `~/.agiworkforce`; a store that is not on the device is skipped. `~/.agiworkforce/security-audit.log` is left out and named with the reason in `export_metadata.left_out_files`: it is a log like the others the export leaves out, each entry a whole command line with its arguments, and a command that spans lines cannot be told from the next entry, so it cannot be reduced to programs the way `command_history` is. It is written by `execute_terminal_command` (one-shot commands); `command_history` is written by interactive sessions only, so one-shot commands are in no export. Deleting clears web data through the webview and removes the rest at the next launch; `~/.agiworkforce` stays, except that log, because the command-line tool owns it too.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `write_shared_config`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Gate        | Developer settings calls it (`DotfileSettings.tsx` through the command client), so it stays. It replaced any top-level key of `~/.agiworkforce/config.toml` with whatever the window sent, `providers.*.base_url` included. It now saves the `default` table only, every name in it must be a setting the command-line tool's `DefaultConfig` has (`DEFAULT_SETTINGS`; `SharedDefaultConfig` gained `reasoning_effort`, `permission_mode`, `mcp_initialize_timeout` and `mcp_call_tool_timeout` to match, and a test ties the list to the struct) and every value must parse as that type. A save that makes `approval_mode`, `permission_mode` or `sandbox_mode` allow more is written only after a yes in `sys::native_confirm` naming each as it is and as it would be; `false` is a decline and nothing is written. Order, least to most: approval `suggest`/`ask`, `auto-edit`, `full-auto`; permission `plan`, `default`, `acceptEdits`, `dontAsk`; sandbox `read-only`, `workspace`/`workspace-write`/`contained`, `full-auto`, `off`/`danger-full-access`/`unrestricted`, compared without case, `-` or `_`. A value on no ladder asks when moved to and not when moved away from; removing `sandbox_mode` asks, because what the tool does without it was not established. An unchanged or tighter value never asks.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `dotfile_add_mcp_server`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Gate        | Live through the same page. It wrote any JSON under any name into `~/.agiworkforce/mcp.json`, and the command-line tool starts what that file names as the user's own server, outside its sandbox (`apps/cli/src/mcp/mod.rs`, `user_servers`). It now takes a command, its arguments and its environment and nothing else: any other key (`transport`, `url`, `allowLocalNetwork`, the CLI's `sandbox`) is refused, because the dialog would not show it. The entry is written only after a yes in `sys::native_confirm` showing the name, the whole command, every argument and each variable as `agiworkforce_sandbox_policy::shown_value` shows it (a credential by name is never shown), and saying whether it replaces an entry; a launch one dialog cannot show whole is refused. `false` is a decline. `dotfile_remove_mcp_server` is unchanged in Rust and now asks through `useConfirmAction` in the page, where it fired straight from the click. `dotfile_list_mcp_servers`, `dotfile_list_skills`, `dotfile_read_instructions`, `dotfile_read_memories` and `read_shared_config` are unchanged. `dotfile_write_instructions` stays ungated; the residual is `DESKTOP-STANDING-INSTRUCTIONS-WINDOW-WRITABLE-01` in `audit/registers/known-flaws.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `load_shared_config`, `SharedConfig`, `SharedProviderConfig`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Cut         | The typed reader of the shared config "for startup" had no caller anywhere in `apps/desktop/src-tauri`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `memory_delete_all`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | Gate        | Deleted every memory on a call from the window with only the in-window confirmation. It now lists what is there, asks through `sys::native_confirm` behind a `ConfirmGate` ("Delete all N memories?", consenting button "Delete All Memories"), and deletes exactly the memories it listed; it returns `None` for a decline, and the Memory tab then says nothing was deleted. Nothing is asked when there is nothing to delete. `memory_delete` and `memory_forget_topic` remove one memory and stay in-window, as does `chat_delete_conversation`: there is no delete-all for conversations in Rust or in the page.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Consent to a project's skills in the Tauri app                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Wire, moved | It was a `.consent` file inside the project's skills folder, written by `skill_set_workspace_consent` on a call from the window, so the project, or a model edit, could consent for itself. It is now a `settings_v2` row of the `security` category, kept as project MCP approvals are: key `skill_project_consent_` and the SHA-256 of the resolved skills folder, value a digest of every project skill as it is loaded (path, name, description, instructions, requirements, supported systems, allowed tools, context mode), which is what the model is given. `skill_set_workspace_consent(true)` builds the question first, asks through `sys::native_confirm` behind a `ConfirmGate` listing every skill by name, and records the digest that was listed; a list one dialog cannot show is refused. A skill added, removed or edited no longer matches and is withheld until asked again (`changedSinceConsent`). Nothing in the project is read as consent. Two paths that handed out a project skill's instructions with no consent check now go through the same check: `skill_get_instructions` (a skill chosen in the composer is put in the custom instructions) and `skill_invoke` (the app's own MCP server). Sibling files in a skill's folder are not part of the digest: a script the instructions name is run through the terminal tool and its approvals. The row is left out of the local export with the rest of the `security` category.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Secrets handed to an MCP server                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Wire        | A server that names `<from_api_key:…>` or `<from_oauth:…>` is handed the user's provider key or sign-in token. `mcp_list_servers` now sends those references, read from the configuration and never from a value, and `MCPServerCard` says so at the Enabled switch, which is where consent to a project server is recorded: "Turning this on gives this server your Anthropic API key."                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Unused command-client wrappers (361 of 420)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Cut         | Their commands are live through direct `invoke` calls in the renderer; nothing referenced the wrappers. Twenty modules are left empty and deleted (`analytics`, `artifacts`, `capabilities`, `cloudStorage`, `customAgents`, `customInstructions`, `database`, `document`, `errorReporting`, `fileOps`, `governance`, `models`, `ollama`, `productivity`, `projects`, `research`, `scheduler`, `security`, `thinking`, `toolConfirmation`); 17 modules and 59 wrappers remain. | Unreferenced renderer functions (49)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Cut         | Exported from `api/agi.ts`, `analytics.ts`, `browser.ts`, `codeEditing.ts`, `codeSearch.ts`, `git.ts`, `reflection.ts`, `toolConfirmation.ts` and `undo.ts`, plus the unused `useChatPreferencesStore`. Each called `invoke` and nothing referenced it; 19 of them called commands that are live elsewhere. ## 2026-10-11: project MCP servers and kept transcripts

| Capability                                                           | Decision                                             | Evidence and retained path                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Approving a project MCP server from a client that has no terminal    | Wire                                                 | The only approval surface was `mcp::consent::review_project_servers`, a terminal prompt at interactive start, so a server the app-server withheld could never be approved from Electron or VS Code. `mcp/approval/read {name, scope}` returns the question and the launch fingerprint computed by the host; `mcp/approval/grant {name, scope, fingerprint}` records consent only when the launch still hashes to that fingerprint, so a client approves exactly what it was shown. The read also returns `launch`, the same launch in parts (program, arguments, environment names with non-secret values, working folder, pinned files, sandbox, network, project), built beside the fingerprint from the same inputs, and the terminal prompt prints those lines above its question. A grant whose fingerprint no longer matches is refused with its own code, `MCP_APPROVAL_STALE_ERROR_CODE` (-32012), carrying the launch as it is now. Capability `mcpApproval`. `mcp/login` refuses a server that is still awaiting approval. The Electron shell handler is not built yet: until it is, `needs_approval` rows are shown and approval happens in the terminal.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `mcp::consent::record_consent`, `mcp::consent::launch_fingerprint`   | Cut                                                  | No caller. Consent is recorded through `record_as` with the containment decided for that launch, and the fingerprint through `fingerprint_as`; the two free functions hashed a launch without its containment.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `.vscode/mcp.json` in `protected_paths.rs`                           | Keep                                                 | The CLI never loads it. It is protected because other tools on the same machine do: a sandboxed command that could write it would plant a server for VS Code to start. Protection follows what any tool loads, not what this one loads.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Where a project MCP server starts                                    | Wire                                                 | A stdio server is started in the project the session is rooted at, passed down from `build_mcp_manager_in` through `McpManager::start_servers_in`, not in the directory the CLI process happens to run in. The folder travels as `TransportConfig::Stdio.cwd` and the spawn in `agiworkforce-mcp` sets it, so one mechanism holds on every platform and for a sandbox wrapper as for the server it runs; the `sh -c cd` wrapper is cut. A relative command is read from that folder. A stdio server with no project is refused. A project root no sandbox may write gets a private temporary workspace for the connection.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Pinning the program a bare command resolves to                       | Wire                                                 | `Launch::Stdio.program` holds the path a bare command resolves to on the host `PATH`, and the launch runs that path, so a later `PATH` change or a same-named file earlier on it changes the fingerprint and asks again.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Which launch files an approval pins                                  | Wire, shared                                         | The step that turns a launch into pinned content, which files it runs that a sandbox could have written and a digest of each, was written once in the CLI and once in the Tauri sandbox opt-out. It is now `agiworkforce_sandbox_policy::launch_files::pinned_launch_file_digests(project, starts_in_project, command, args, package_entries, digest)`, returning label to digest in label order. The crate has neither a JSON parser nor a hash and gains none, so the lockfile is unchanged: the host passes its `package.json` reader and its digest. The CLI passes the SHA-256 hex it always used, so every recorded approval still matches. The Tauri project consent can add the same map to its fingerprint; its sandbox opt-out still has its own copy of the step.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Restarting a stopped MCP server                                      | Wire                                                 | `agiworkforce-mcp` restarted a stdio server from the launch it saved, with no check, on the first tool call after a crash. `ClientHooks.before_respawn: Option<RespawnCheck>` is asked first; `Err` keeps the server down and is the whole tool error. The CLI supplies it for servers a project or a project plugin declares: the launch must still hash to what the user approved (`consent::respawn_allowed`), so an edited launch file stays off and `mcp/list` reports `needs_approval`. A server from the user's own config or an installed plugin restarts unchecked, as it starts unchecked: nothing was approved by hash and its entry lives where no sandboxed command writes.
